+     * zabbix-agent(매칭+신선) / cloudwatch(CW 가용) / unavailable(권한·예산·수집 실패) /
+     * unmonitored(중지 등 — 현재 지표가 어디에도 없음). AWS 추가 호출 0(매처 캐시 + CW 가용성 캐시만).
+     */
+    private String monitoredBy(Instance instance, String region) {
+        java.util.Optional<Ec2ZabbixMatcher.Match> match = matcher.matchFor(instance);
+        if (match.isPresent() && matcher.isFresh(match.get(), java.time.Duration.ofMinutes(30))) {
+            return "zabbix-agent";
+        }
+        String state = instance.state() == null ? "unknown" : instance.state().nameAsString();
+        if ("stopped".equals(state)) {
+            return "unmonitored";
+        }
+        if ("ok".equals(cloudWatchAvailability.availability(region))) {
+            return "cloudwatch";
+        }
+        return "unavailable";
+    }

+        Map<String, Object> result = cloudWatchSeries(instanceId, region, from, to, now);
+        @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
+        Map<String, Object> collection = (Map<String, Object>) result.get("collection");
+        collection.put("source", "cloudwatch");
+        collection.put("fallbackUsed", match.isPresent());
+        match.ifPresent(m -> collection.put("matchBasis", m.basis()));
+        return result;
+    }
+
+    private Optional<Ec2ZabbixMatcher.Match> findMatch(String instanceId, String region) {
+        try {
+            Instance instance = aws.findInstance(region, instanceId);
+            return matcher.matchFor(instance);
+        } catch (Exception e) {
+            log.debug("ec2 lookup for matching failed ({}): {}", instanceId, e.getMessage());
+            return Optional.empty();
+        }
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * agent 시계열 — CPU util(%)·메모리 pused(%). 1차 범위: 단일 시리즈 확정 지표만
+     * (디스크 사용률·네트워크는 파티션/인터페이스 다차원이라 2차 — 설계 §4).
+     *
+     * @return 품질 게이트(둘 다 존재 + 신선) 미달이면 null → 호출부가 CW fallback.
+     */
+    private Map<String, Object> tryAgentSeries(Ec2ZabbixMatcher.Match match, Instant from, Instant to, Instant now) {
+        try {
+            EntityRef entity = EntityRef.ofDefault(match.hostid());
+            TimeRange range = new TimeRange(from.getEpochSecond(), to.getEpochSecond());
+            // 🔴 시스템 컨텍스트 — 클라우드 화면은 awsResources.view로 게이트되며, 스냅샷 축과 동일하게
+            //    시스템 수집 데이터를 소비한다(사용자별 Zabbix 세션을 태우면 캐시·권한이 얽힌다 — §8).
+            List<Series> cpu = metricPort.querySeries(SRC, CallerContext.system(),
+                    SeriesQuery.of(entity, MetricRef.of(MetricDomain.CPU, "util"), range, Rollup.RAW));
+            List<Series> memory = metricPort.querySeries(SRC, CallerContext.system(),
+                    SeriesQuery.of(entity, MetricRef.of(MetricDomain.MEMORY, "pused"), range, Rollup.RAW));
+
+            long latestEpoch = Math.max(latestEpoch(cpu), latestEpoch(memory));
+            boolean fresh = latestEpoch > 0
+                    && Instant.ofEpochSecond(latestEpoch).isAfter(now.minus(AGENT_FRESHNESS));
+            if (seriesEmpty(cpu) || seriesEmpty(memory) || !fresh) {
+                log.debug("agent series quality gate failed for host {} (fresh={})", match.hostid(), fresh);
+                return null;
+            }
+
+            Map<String, Object> series = emptySeries();
+            series.put("cpu", toPoints(cpu));
+            series.put("memoryUsage", toPoints(memory));
+            Map<String, Object> collection = new LinkedHashMap<>();
+            collection.put("state", "ok");
+            collection.put("reason", null);
+            collection.put("source", "zabbix-agent");
+            collection.put("fallbackUsed", false);
+            collection.put("matchBasis", match.basis());
+            collection.put("zabbixHostid", match.hostid());
+            collection.put("zabbixHostName", match.hostName());
+            collection.put("observedAt", Instant.ofEpochSecond(latestEpoch).toString());
+            return Map.of("items", series, "collection", collection);
+        } catch (Exception e) {
+            // Zabbix 장애도 CW fallback 사유다 — 죽지 않는다.
+            log.warn("agent series fetch failed for host {}: {}", match.hostid(), e.getMessage());
+            return null;
+        }
+    }
+

+    private static long latestEpoch(List<Series> series) {

  80:         // 1?쒖쐞: 紐낆떆 ?앸퀎??        AgentHost byId = snapshot.byInstanceId().get(instance.instanceId());
  81:         if (byId != null) {
  82:             return Optional.of(match(byId, BASIS_INSTANCE_ID));
  83:         }
  84:         // 2?쒖쐞: IP ??ENI ?꾩껜 二쇱냼濡??좎씪 ?몄뒪?몃쭔
  85:         Set<AgentHost> hits = new LinkedHashSet<>();
  86:         for (String address : instanceAddresses(instance)) {
  87:             List<AgentHost> candidates = snapshot.byAddress().get(address);
  88:             if (candidates != null) {
  89:                 hits.addAll(candidates);
  90:             }
  91:         }
  92:         if (hits.size() == 1) {
  93:             return Optional.of(match(hits.iterator().next(), BASIS_IP));
  94:         }
  95:         if (hits.size() > 1) {
  96:             log.debug("ec2 {} matches {} zabbix hosts by ip ??not linking", instance.instanceId(), hits.size());
  97:         }
  98:         return Optional.empty();
  99:     }
 100: 
 101:     /** EC2??留ㅼ묶 ?꾨낫 二쇱냼 ?꾩껜 ??primary ??ENI??secondary private IP쨌?곌껐 public IP ?ы븿(肄붾뜳??吏??. */
 102:     static Set<String> instanceAddresses(Instance instance) {
 103:         Set<String> addresses = new LinkedHashSet<>();
 104:         addIfPresent(addresses, instance.privateIpAddress());
 105:         addIfPresent(addresses, instance.publicIpAddress());
 106:         for (var eni : instance.networkInterfaces()) {
 107:             for (var ip : eni.privateIpAddresses()) {
 108:                 addIfPresent(addresses, ip.privateIpAddress());
 109:                 if (ip.association() != null) {
 110:                     addIfPresent(addresses, ip.association().publicIp());
 111:                 }
 112:             }
 113:         }
 114:         return addresses;
 115:     }
 116: 
 117:     private static void addIfPresent(Set<String> into, String address) {
 118:         if (address != null && !address.isBlank()) {
 119:             into.add(address.trim());
 120:         }
 121:     }
 122: 
 123:     private Match match(AgentHost host, String basis) {
 124:         return new Match(host.hostid(), host.name(), basis, host.lastCheckEpoch(), Instant.now());
 125:     }
 126: 
 127:     // ===== Zabbix ?몄뒪??罹먯떆 =====
 128: 
 129:     record AgentHost(String hostid, String name, Set<String> addresses, Long lastCheckEpoch) {
 130:     }
 131: 
 132:     private record Snapshot(Instant fetchedAt, Map<String, List<AgentHost>> byAddress,
 133:             Map<String, AgentHost> byInstanceId) {
 134:     }
 135: 
 136:     private Snapshot hosts() {
 137:         Snapshot current = cache.get();
 138:         if (current != null && current.fetchedAt().isAfter(Instant.now().minus(CACHE_TTL))) {
 139:             return current;
 140:         }
 141:         try {
 142:             List<InventoryHost> hosts = inventoryPort.listHosts(SRC, CallerContext.system(),
 143:                     new HostQuery(null, null, Set.of(HostInclude.TAGS, HostInclude.LAST_CHECK)));
 144:             Map<String, List<AgentHost>> byAddress = new HashMap<>();
 145:             Map<String, AgentHost> byInstanceId = new HashMap<>();
 146:             Set<String> ambiguousInstanceIds = new HashSet<>();
 147:             for (InventoryHost host : hosts) {
 148:                 Set<String> addresses = new LinkedHashSet<>();
 149:                 for (HostNetworkInterface iface : host.interfaces()) {
 150:                     // ?뵶 AGENT ?명꽣?섏씠?ㅻ쭔 ??SNMP/IPMI 二쇱냼濡?"agent ?덉쓬" ?먯젙?섎㈃ ?깅뒫 吏?쒓? ?녿뒗??                    //    ?덈떎怨?留먰븯???덉씠 ?쒕떎(肄붾뜳??吏??.
 151:                     if (iface.kind() == InterfaceKind.AGENT) {
 152:                         addIfPresent(addresses, iface.address());
 153:                     }
 154:                 }
 155:                 if (addresses.isEmpty()) {
 156:                     continue;
 157:                 }
 158:                 AgentHost agentHost = new AgentHost(host.ref().nativeId(), host.name(), addresses, host.lastCheckEpoch());
 159:                 for (String address : addresses) {
 160:                     byAddress.computeIfAbsent(address, k -> new java.util.ArrayList<>()).add(agentHost);
 161:                 }
 162:                 for (String instanceId : instanceIdClaims(host)) {
 163:                     // 媛숈? instanceId瑜????몄뒪?멸? 二쇱옣?섎㈃ ????踰꾨┛?????좎씪??洹쒖튃.
 164:                     if (byInstanceId.put(instanceId, agentHost) != null) {
 165:                         ambiguousInstanceIds.add(instanceId);
 166:                     }
 167:                 }
 168:             }
 169:             ambiguousInstanceIds.forEach(byInstanceId::remove);
 170:             Snapshot fresh = new Snapshot(Instant.now(), byAddress, byInstanceId);
 171:             cache.set(fresh);
 172:             return fresh;
 173:         } catch (Exception e) {
 174:             log.warn("zabbix host fetch for ec2 matching failed: {}", e.getMessage());
 175:             // ?≪? 罹먯떆?쇰룄 ?덉쑝硫??대떎 ??留ㅼ묶???좉퉸 ?〓뒗 寃껋씠 留ㅼ묶 ?꾨㈇蹂대떎 ?ル떎.
 176:             return current;
 177:         }
 178:     }
 179: 
 180:     /** ?몄뒪?멸? 二쇱옣?섎뒗 instanceId ???몄뒪?몃챸??instanceId ?뺤떇?닿굅?? aws.instanceId ?쒓렇. */
 181:     private static Set<String> instanceIdClaims(InventoryHost host) {
 182:         Set<String> claims = new LinkedHashSet<>();
 183:         if (host.name() != null && host.name().startsWith("i-")) {
 184:             claims.add(host.name().trim());
 185:         }
 186:         host.tags().stream()
 187:                 .filter(tag -> TAG_INSTANCE_ID.equals(tag.name()))
 188:                 .map(tag -> tag.value())
 189:                 .filter(v -> v != null && !v.isBlank())
 190:                 .forEach(v -> claims.add(v.trim()));
 191:         return claims;
 192:     }
 193: }

  19: @Component
  20: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  21: public class CloudWatchAvailability {
  22: 
  23:     /** 沅뚰븳 嫄곕? ???ъ떆???듭젣 ?쒓컙 ??留?議고쉶媛 嫄곕??뱁븯硫?濡쒓렇瑜?梨꾩슦??寃껋쓣 留됰뒗?? */
  24:     private static final Duration DENIED_TTL = Duration.ofMinutes(10);
  25: 
  26:     private final CloudApiBudgetRepository budgetRepository;
  27: 
  28:     /** ?곕━ 紐????곹븳(D3: ?꾨━?곗뼱???덈컲). */
  29:     @Value("${app.cloud.metrics-monthly-call-cap:500000}")
  30:     private long monthlyCallCap;
  31: 
  32:     private final Map<String, Instant> deniedUntilByRegion = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
  33: 
  34:     public void markDenied(String region) {
  35:         deniedUntilByRegion.put(region, Instant.now().plus(DENIED_TTL));
  36:     }
  37: 
  38:     public boolean isDenied(String region) {
  39:         Instant until = deniedUntilByRegion.get(region);
  40:         return until != null && Instant.now().isBefore(until);
  41:     }
  42: 
  43:     /** ?대쾲 ???ъ슜??議고쉶(?덉궛 ?쒖떆쨌媛??怨듭슜). */
  44:     public long usedThisMonth() {
  45:         String month = LocalDate.now().format(DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyy-MM"));
  46:         return budgetRepository.findById(month).map(b -> b.getCalls()).orElse(0L);
  47:     }
  48: 
  49:     public long cap() {
  50:         return monthlyCallCap;
  51:     }
  52: 
  53:     /** ?덉궛 ?뚯쭊 ?щ? ??nextCalls留뚰겮 ??遺瑜??ъ쑀媛 ?녿뒗媛. */
  54:     public boolean budgetExhausted(int nextCalls) {
  55:         return usedThisMonth() + nextCalls > monthlyCallCap;
  56:     }
  57: 
  58:     /**
  59:      * 而ㅻ쾭由ъ? ?쒖떆???붿빟 ?먯젙: 'ok' | 'denied' | 'budget'.
  60:      * ?좑툘 AWS瑜??몄텧?섏? ?딅뒗????罹먯떆쨌移댁슫?곕쭔 蹂몃떎(?곹깭 ?먯젙 臾댄샇異??먯튃怨??숈씪).
  61:      */
  62:     public String availability(String region) {
  63:         if (isDenied(region)) {
  64:             return "denied";
  65:         }
  66:         if (budgetExhausted(1)) {
  67:             return "budget";
  68:         }
  69:         return "ok";
  70:     }
  71: }

  50:             new MetricSpec("networkOut", "NetworkOut", Statistic.AVERAGE),

  56:

  59:     /** agent ?덉쭏 寃뚯씠????留덉?留??ъ씤?멸? ???덉씠?댁빞 "?좎꽑"?대떎(?섏쭛 吏?걔룹젙吏 媛먯?). */
  60:     private static final Duration AGENT_FRESHNESS = Duration.ofMinutes(30);
  61:     private static final SourceRef SRC = SourceRef.DEFAULT;
  62: 
  63:     private final AwsReadOnlyGateway aws;
  64:     private final CloudApiBudgetRepository budgetRepository;
  65:     private final CloudWatchAvailability availability;
  66:     private final Ec2ZabbixMatcher matcher;
  67:     private final MetricPort metricPort;
  68: 
  69:     /**
  70:      * ?몄뒪?댁뒪 1????깅뒫 ?쒓퀎?? from/to??epoch seconds, 0?대㈃ 理쒓렐 24?쒓컙.
  71:      * ?ㅽ뙣 紐⑤뱶???꾨? 200 + 鍮?series?????듯빀 ??꾨씪?몄뿉???깅뒫 異뺣쭔 議곗슜??鍮좎쭊??
  72:      */
  73:     public Map<String, Object> metrics(String instanceId, String region, long fromEpoch, long toEpoch) {
  74:         Instant now = Instant.now();
  75:         Instant to = toEpoch > 0 ? Instant.ofEpochSecond(toEpoch) : now;
  76:         Instant from = fromEpoch > 0 ? Instant.ofEpochSecond(fromEpoch) : to.minus(Duration.ofDays(1));
  77: 
  78:         // ?? 1) Zabbix agent 寃쎈줈 ??留ㅼ묶 + ?덉쭏 寃뚯씠???듦낵 ??CW瑜?遺瑜댁? ?딅뒗???꾨━?곗뼱 ?덉빟)
  79:         Optional<Ec2ZabbixMatcher.Match> match = findMatch(instanceId, region);
  80:         if (match.isPresent()) {
  81:             Map<String, Object> agent = tryAgentSeries(match.get(), from, to, now);
  82:             if (agent != null) {
  83:                 return agent;
  84:             }
  85:         }
  86: 
  87:         // ?? 2) CloudWatch 寃쎈줈(?꾪뻾) ??留ㅼ묶?먮뒗???ш린濡??붾떎硫?紐낆떆??fallback?대떎
  88:         Map<String, Object> result = cloudWatchSeries(instanceId, region, from, to, now);
  89:         @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
  90:         Map<String, Object> collection = (Map<String, Object>) result.get("collection");
  91:         collection.put("source", "cloudwatch");
  92:         collection.put("fallbackUsed", match.isPresent());
  93:         match.ifPresent(m -> collection.put("matchBasis", m.basis()));
  94:         return result;
  95:     }
  96: 
  97:     private Optional<Ec2ZabbixMatcher.Match> findMatch(String instanceId, String region) {
  98:         try {
  99:             Instance instance = aws.findInstance(region, instanceId);
 100:             return matcher.matchFor(instance);
 101:         } catch (Exception e) {
 102:             log.debug("ec2 lookup for matching failed ({}): {}", instanceId, e.getMessage());
 103:             return Optional.empty();
 104:         }
 105:     }
 106: 
 107:     /**
 108:      * agent ?쒓퀎????CPU util(%)쨌硫붾え由?pused(%). 1李?踰붿쐞: ?⑥씪 ?쒕━利??뺤젙 吏?쒕쭔
 109:      * (?붿뒪???ъ슜瑜졖룸꽕?몄썙?щ뒗 ?뚰떚???명꽣?섏씠???ㅼ감?먯씠??2李????ㅺ퀎 짠4).
 110:      *
 111:      * @return ?덉쭏 寃뚯씠??????議댁옱 + ?좎꽑) 誘몃떖?대㈃ null ???몄텧遺媛 CW fallback.
 112:      */
 113:     private Map<String, Object> tryAgentSeries(Ec2ZabbixMatcher.Match match, Instant from, Instant to, Instant now) {
 114:         try {
 115:             EntityRef entity = EntityRef.ofDefault(match.hostid());
 116:             TimeRange range = new TimeRange(from.getEpochSecond(), to.getEpochSecond());
 117:             // ?뵶 ?쒖뒪??而⑦뀓?ㅽ듃 ???대씪?곕뱶 ?붾㈃? awsResources.view濡?寃뚯씠?몃릺硫? ?ㅻ깄??異뺢낵 ?숈씪?섍쾶
 118:             //    ?쒖뒪???섏쭛 ?곗씠?곕? ?뚮퉬?쒕떎(?ъ슜?먮퀎 Zabbix ?몄뀡???쒖슦硫?罹먯떆쨌沅뚰븳???쏀엺????짠8).
 119:             List<Series> cpu = metricPort.querySeries(SRC, CallerContext.system(),
 120:                     SeriesQuery.of(entity, MetricRef.of(MetricDomain.CPU, "util"), range, Rollup.RAW));
 121:             List<Series> memory = metricPort.querySeries(SRC, CallerContext.system(),
 122:                     SeriesQuery.of(entity, MetricRef.of(MetricDomain.MEMORY, "pused"), range, Rollup.RAW));
 123: 
 124:             long latestEpoch = Math.max(latestEpoch(cpu), latestEpoch(memory));
 125:             boolean fresh = latestEpoch > 0
 126:                     && Instant.ofEpochSecond(latestEpoch).isAfter(now.minus(AGENT_FRESHNESS));
 127:             if (seriesEmpty(cpu) || seriesEmpty(memory) || !fresh) {
 128:                 log.debug("agent series quality gate failed for host {} (fresh={})", match.hostid(), fresh);
 129:                 return null;
 130:             }
 131: 
 132:             Map<String, Object> series = emptySeries();
 133:             series.put("cpu", toPoints(cpu));
 134:             series.put("memoryUsage", toPoints(memory));
 135:             Map<String, Object> collection = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 136:             collection.put("state", "ok");
 137:             collection.put("reason", null);
 138:             collection.put("source", "zabbix-agent");
 139:             collection.put("fallbackUsed", false);
 140:             collection.put("matchBasis", match.basis());
 141:             collection.put("zabbixHostid", match.hostid());
 142:             collection.put("zabbixHostName", match.hostName());
 143:             collection.put("observedAt", Instant.ofEpochSecond(latestEpoch).toString());
 144:             return Map.of("items", series, "collection", collection);
 145:         } catch (Exception e) {
 146:             // Zabbix ?μ븷??CW fallback ?ъ쑀????二쎌? ?딅뒗??
 147:             log.warn("agent series fetch failed for host {}: {}", match.hostid(), e.getMessage());
 148:             return null;
 149:         }
 150:     }
 151: 
 152:     private Map<String, Object> cloudWatchSeries(String instanceId, String region, Instant from, Instant to,
 153:             Instant now) {
 154:         if (availability.isDenied(region)) {
 155:             return degraded("unavailable", "CLOUDWATCH_ACCESS_DENIED");
 156:         }
 157:         if (availability.budgetExhausted(CW_METRICS.size())) {
 158:             log.warn("cloud metrics budget exhausted: used={} cap={}", availability.usedThisMonth(),
 159:                     availability.cap());
 160:             return degraded("disabled", "BUDGET_EXHAUSTED");
 161:         }
 162:         long used = availability.usedThisMonth();
 163:         int period = resolvePeriodSeconds(from, to, now);
 164:         Map<String, Object> series = emptySeries();
 165:         int calls = 0;
 166:         try {
 167:             for (MetricSpec metric : CW_METRICS) {
 168:                 calls++;
 169:                 List<Datapoint> points = aws.getMetricStatistics(region, NAMESPACE, metric.metricName(),
 170:                         "InstanceId", instanceId, from, to, period, metric.statistic());
 171:                 series.put(metric.key(), points.stream()
 172:                         // 臾몄꽌 紐낆떆: ?곗씠?고룷?명듃???쒓컙?쒖씠 ?꾨땲?????ш린???뺣젹??FE 遺?댁쓣 ?놁븻??
 173:                         .sorted(Comparator.comparing(Datapoint::timestamp))
 174:                         .map(p -> Map.of("time", p.timestamp().toString(),
 175:                                 "value", statisticValue(p, metric.statistic())))
 176:                         .toList());
 177:             }
 178:         } catch (AwsServiceException e) {
 179:             recordCalls(calls);
 180:             if (isAccessDenied(e)) {
 181:                 // ???곕뒗 怨좉컼? ?뺤긽 ?곹깭 ??由ъ쟾 ?⑥쐞 TTL 罹먯떆濡??ъ떆?꾨? 硫덉텛怨?議곗슜??媛뺣벑?쒕떎.
 182:                 availability.markDenied(region);
 183:                 log.info("CloudWatch access denied in {} ??metrics degraded: {}", region, e.getMessage());
 184:                 return degraded("unavailable", "CLOUDWATCH_ACCESS_DENIED");
 185:             }
 186:             log.warn("CloudWatch metrics unavailable in {}: {}", region, e.getMessage());
 187:             return degraded("unavailable", "CLOUDWATCH_UNAVAILABLE");
 188:         }
 189:         recordCalls(calls);
 190:         Map<String, Object> collection = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 191:         collection.put("state", "ok");
 192:         collection.put("reason", null);
 193:         collection.put("periodSeconds", period);
 194:         collection.put("budget", Map.of("used", used + calls, "cap", availability.cap()));
 195:         Map<String, Object> result = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 196:         result.put("items", series);
 197:         result.put("collection", collection);
 198:         return result;
 199:     }
 200: 
 201:     /**
 202:      * Period ?곗젙 ??議고쉶 湲곌컙怨?CloudWatch retention 洹쒖튃???④퍡 留뚯”?쒗궓??
 203:      * 洹쒖튃(怨듭떇 臾몄꽌): ?쒖옉??15???댁쟾?대㈃ 300s 諛곗닔, 63???댁쟾?대㈃ 3600s 諛곗닔. ?ш린??     * "?몄텧??理쒕? 1,440 ?ъ씤??瑜?base 諛곗닔 ?뺣?濡?吏?⑤떎.
 204:      */
 205:     static int resolvePeriodSeconds(Instant from, Instant to, Instant now) {
 206:         int base = from.isAfter(now.minus(Duration.ofDays(63))) ? 300 : 3600;
 207:         long rangeSeconds = Math.max(1, Duration.between(from, to).getSeconds());
 208:         long minPeriod = (long) Math.ceil(rangeSeconds / (double) MAX_DATAPOINTS);
 209:         long multiples = Math.max(1, (long) Math.ceil(minPeriod / (double) base));
 210:         return (int) (multiples * base);
 211:     }
 212: 
 213:     private void recordCalls(int calls) {
 214:         if (calls > 0) {
 215:             budgetRepository.addCalls(LocalDate.now().format(DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyy-MM")), calls);
 216:         }
 217:     }
 218: 
 219:     private static boolean isAccessDenied(AwsServiceException e) {
 220:         String code = e.awsErrorDetails() == null ? null : e.awsErrorDetails().errorCode();
 221:         return e.statusCode() == 403
 222:                 || "AccessDenied".equals(code) || "AccessDeniedException".equals(code)
 223:                 || "UnauthorizedOperation".equals(code);
 224:     }
 225: 
 226:     private static double statisticValue(Datapoint p, Statistic statistic) {
 227:         Double value = statistic == Statistic.AVERAGE ? p.average()
 228:                 : statistic == Statistic.MAXIMUM ? p.maximum()
 229:                         : statistic == Statistic.MINIMUM ? p.minimum() : p.sum();
 230:         return value == null ? 0d : value;
 231:     }
 232: 
 233:     private static boolean seriesEmpty(List<Series> series) {
 234:         return series.isEmpty() || series.get(0).points().isEmpty();
 235:     }
 236: 
 237:     private static long latestEpoch(List<Series> series) {
 238:         return series.stream().flatMap(s -> s.points().stream())
 239:                 .mapToLong(p -> p.epoch()).max().orElse(0L);
 240:     }
 241: 
 242:     private static List<Map<String, Object>> toPoints(List<Series> series) {
 243:         return series.get(0).points().stream()
 244:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparingLong(p -> p.epoch()))
 245:                 .map(p -> Map.<String, Object>of("time", Instant.ofEpochSecond(p.epoch()).toString(),
 246:                         "value", p.value()))
 247:                 .toList();
 248:     }
 249: 
 250:     private static Map<String, Object> emptySeries() {
 251:         Map<String, Object> series = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 252:         for (String key : ALL_KEYS) {
 253:             series.put(key, List.of());
 254:         }
 255:         return series;
 256:     }
 257: 
 258:     /** 媛뺣벑 ?묐떟 ??紐⑤뱺 吏???ㅻ? 鍮?諛곗뿴濡??좎???FE媛 ??議댁옱 ?щ?濡?遺꾧린?섏? ?딄쾶 ?쒕떎. */
 259:     private static Map<String, Object> degraded(String state, String reason) {
 260:         Map<String, Object> collection = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 261:         collection.put("state", state);
 262:         collection.put("reason", reason);
 263:         Map<String, Object> result = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 264:         result.put("items", emptySeries());
 265:         result.put("collection", collection);
 266:         return result;
 267:     }
 268: 
 269:     private record MetricSpec(String key, String metricName, Statistic statistic) {
 270:     }
 271: }

  38:     /** 由ъ쟾 ?ъ븘??吏??蹂묐젹 ?몄텧 ?곹븳(由ъ쟾??留롮븘???ㅻ젅????＜ 諛⑹?). */
  39:     private static final int MAX_PARALLELISM = 16;
  40: 
  41:     private final AwsReadOnlyGateway aws;
  42:     private final CloudSnapshotQueryService snapshotQuery;
  43:     private final Ec2ZabbixMatcher matcher;
  44:     private final CloudWatchAvailability cloudWatchAvailability;
  45: 
  46:     /**
  47:      * 紐⑤땲?곕쭅 而ㅻ쾭由ъ? ?먯젙(?뚯뒪 留ㅽ듃由?뒪, devdesign_ec2-monitoring-sources.md 짠4 ??4媛?:
  48:      * zabbix-agent(留ㅼ묶+?좎꽑) / cloudwatch(CW 媛?? / unavailable(沅뚰븳쨌?덉궛쨌?섏쭛 ?ㅽ뙣) /
  49:      * unmonitored(以묒? ?????꾩옱 吏?쒓? ?대뵒?먮룄 ?놁쓬). AWS 異붽? ?몄텧 0(留ㅼ쿂 罹먯떆 + CW 媛?⑹꽦 罹먯떆留?.
  50:      */
  51:     private String monitoredBy(Instance instance, String region) {
  52:         java.util.Optional<Ec2ZabbixMatcher.Match> match = matcher.matchFor(instance);
  53:         if (match.isPresent() && matcher.isFresh(match.get(), java.time.Duration.ofMinutes(30))) {
  54:             return "zabbix-agent";
  55:         }
  56:         String state = instance.state() == null ? "unknown" : instance.state().nameAsString();
  57:         if ("stopped".equals(state)) {
  58:             return "unmonitored";
  59:         }
  60:         if ("ok".equals(cloudWatchAvailability.availability(region))) {
  61:             return "cloudwatch";
  62:         }
  63:         return "unavailable";
  64:     }
  65: 
  66:     /**
  67:      * ?щ윭 ?묒뾽???쒗븳???ㅻ젅?쒗??먯꽌 蹂묐젹 ?ㅽ뻾?섍퀬 ?쒖텧 ?쒖꽌?濡?寃곌낵瑜?紐⑥???
  68:      * 由ъ쟾 ?ъ븘??由ъ쟾??AWS ?몄텧 ?섏떗 ?????쒖감?믩퀝?щ줈 諛붽퓭 踰쎌떆怨??쒓컙???⑷퀎?먯꽌 理쒕? 1嫄댁쑝濡??⑥텞.
  69:      * 媛쒕퀎 ?묒뾽 ?ㅽ뙣??null濡??섏쭛(?몄텧遺?먯꽌 skip). ?묒뾽 肄쒕갚? ?먯껜?곸쑝濡??덉쇅瑜?泥섎━??遺遺꾧컪??諛섑솚?섎뒗 寃껋쓣 沅뚯옣.
  70:      */
  71:     private <T> List<T> runParallel(List<? extends Callable<T>> tasks) {
  72:         if (tasks.isEmpty()) {
  73:             return List.of();
  74:         }
  75:         if (tasks.size() == 1) {
  76:             try {
  77:                 return new ArrayList<>(List.of(tasks.get(0).call()));
  78:             } catch (Exception e) {
  79:                 log.error("parallel task failed: {}", e.getMessage());
  80:                 List<T> single = new ArrayList<>();
  81:                 single.add(null);
  82:                 return single;
  83:             }
  84:         }
  85:         ExecutorService pool = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(Math.min(tasks.size(), MAX_PARALLELISM));
  86:         try {
  87:             List<Future<T>> futures = new ArrayList<>();
  88:             for (Callable<T> task : tasks) {
  89:                 futures.add(pool.submit(task));
  90:             }
  91:             List<T> results = new ArrayList<>();
  92:             for (Future<T> f : futures) {
  93:                 try {
  94:                     results.add(f.get());
  95:                 } catch (Exception e) {
  96:                     log.error("parallel task failed: {}", e.getMessage());
  97:                     results.add(null);
  98:                 }
  99:             }
 100:             return results;
 101:         } finally {
 102:             pool.shutdown();
 103:         }
 104:     }

 107:     public List<String> getRegions() {

 111:                 .toList();
 112:     }

 114:     @Override
 115:     public Map<String, Object> getVpcs(String keyword, List<String> regions) {
 116:         List<String> listRegion = regions != null ? regions : getRegions();
 117:         List<Callable<List<Map<String, Object>>>> tasks = new ArrayList<>();
 118:         for (String region : listRegion) {
 119:             tasks.add(() -> vpcsForRegion(region, keyword));
 120:         }
 121:         List<Map<String, Object>> vpcs = new ArrayList<>();
 122:         for (List<Map<String, Object>> part : runParallel(tasks)) {
 123:             if (part != null) {
 124:                 vpcs.addAll(part);
 125:             }
 126:         }
 127:         return Map.of("items", vpcs);
 128:     }
 129: 
 130:     /** ?⑥씪 由ъ쟾??VPC 紐⑸줉. AWS ?몄텧 ?ㅽ뙣??濡쒓렇 ??鍮?紐⑸줉(?먮낯??region 猷⑦봽 ??skip 嫄곕룞 ?좎?). */
 131:     private List<Map<String, Object>> vpcsForRegion(String region, String keyword) {
 132:         List<Map<String, Object>> vpcs = new ArrayList<>();
 133:         try {
 134:             List<Vpc> vpcList = aws.describeVpcs(region);
 135:             if (vpcList.isEmpty()) {
 136:                 return vpcs;
 137:             }
 138:             List<Subnet> subnets = aws.describeSubnets(region);
 139:             List<Instance> instances = aws.describeInstances(region);
 140: 
 141:             // vpcId ??subnet??mapPublicIpOnLaunch 紐⑸줉
 142:             Map<String, List<Boolean>> subnetMap = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 143:             for (Subnet s : subnets) {
 144:                 if (s.vpcId() != null) {
 145:                     subnetMap.computeIfAbsent(s.vpcId(), k -> new ArrayList<>())
 146:                             .add(Boolean.TRUE.equals(s.mapPublicIpOnLaunch()));
 147:                 }
 148:             }
 149:             // vpcId ??instance ??
 150:             Map<String, Integer> instanceCount = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 151:             for (Instance i : instances) {
 152:                 if (i.vpcId() != null) {
 153:                     instanceCount.merge(i.vpcId(), 1, Integer::sum);
 154:                 }
 155:             }
 156: 
 157:             for (Vpc vpc : vpcList) {
 158:                 String vpcName = vpc.tags().stream().filter(t -> "Name".equals(t.key()))
 159:                         .map(t -> t.value()).findFirst().orElse("-");
 160:                 if (keyword != null && !vpcName.toLowerCase().contains(keyword.toLowerCase())) {
 161:                     continue;
 162:                 }
 163:                 List<Boolean> subList = subnetMap.get(vpc.vpcId());
 164:                 String blockPublicAccess = (subList != null && subList.stream().allMatch(b -> !b)) ? "Off" : "On";
 165: 
 166:                 Map<String, Object> item = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 167:                 item.put("vpcId", vpc.vpcId());
 168:                 item.put("name", vpcName);
 169:                 item.put("ownerId", vpc.ownerId());
 170:                 item.put("region", region);
 171:                 item.put("state", vpc.stateAsString());
 172:                 item.put("ec2Count", instanceCount.getOrDefault(vpc.vpcId(), 0));
 173:                 item.put("ipv4Cidr", vpc.cidrBlock());
 174:                 item.put("ipv6Cidr", vpc.ipv6CidrBlockAssociationSet().stream()
 175:                         .map(x -> x.ipv6CidrBlock()).toList());
 176:                 item.put("tenancy", vpc.instanceTenancyAsString());
 177:                 item.put("blockPublicAccess", blockPublicAccess);
 178:                 vpcs.add(item);
 179:             }
 180:         } catch (Exception e) {
 181:             log.error("Error get VPC on region {}: {}", region, e.getMessage());
 182:         }
 183:         return vpcs;
 184:     }

 202:     /** ?⑥씪 由ъ쟾??EC2 ?곸꽭 紐⑸줉. */
 203:     private List<Map<String, Object>> instancesForRegion(String region, List<String> vpcIds) {
 204:         List<Map<String, Object>> ec2s = new ArrayList<>();
 205:         try {
 206:             List<Instance> instances = aws.describeInstances(region);
 207:             if (instances.isEmpty()) {
 208:                 return ec2s;
 209:             }
 210:             List<String> instanceIds = instances.stream().map(Instance::instanceId)
 211:                     .filter(java.util.Objects::nonNull).toList();
 212:             Set<String> vpcIdSet = new LinkedHashSet<>();
 213:             instances.forEach(i -> {
 214:                 if (i.vpcId() != null) {
 215:                     vpcIdSet.add(i.vpcId());
 216:                 }
 217:             });
 218: 
 219:             Map<String, String> statusMap = instanceStatuses(region, instanceIds);
 220:             Map<String, String> vpcNameMap = vpcNameMap(region, new ArrayList<>(vpcIdSet));
 221: 
 222:             for (Instance inst : instances) {
 223:                 if (vpcIds != null && (inst.vpcId() == null || !vpcIds.contains(inst.vpcId()))) {
 224:                     continue;
 225:                 }
 226:                 String vpcId = inst.vpcId() != null ? inst.vpcId() : "-";
 227:                 String name = inst.tags().stream().filter(t -> "Name".equals(t.key()))
 228:                         .map(t -> t.value()).findFirst().orElse("-");
 229: 
 230:                 Map<String, Object> item = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 231:                 item.put("instanceName", name);
 232:                 item.put("instanceId", inst.instanceId());
 233:                 item.put("instanceState", inst.state() != null ? inst.state().nameAsString() : "-");
 234:                 item.put("instanceType", inst.instanceTypeAsString() != null ? inst.instanceTypeAsString() : "-");
 235:                 item.put("statusCheck", statusMap.getOrDefault(inst.instanceId(), "-"));
 236:                 item.put("region", region);
 237:                 item.put("availabilityZone", inst.placement() != null ? inst.placement().availabilityZone() : "-");
 238:                 item.put("vpcName", vpcNameMap.getOrDefault(vpcId, vpcId));
 239:                 item.put("vpcId", vpcId);
 240:                 item.put("securityGroupName", inst.securityGroups().stream()
 241:                         .map(g -> g.groupName()).reduce((a, b) -> a + ", " + b).orElse("-"));
 242:                 // ?뚯뒪 留ㅽ듃由?뒪 而ㅻ쾭由ъ?(4媛? ??"?????몄뒪?댁뒪??硫붾え由?룸뵒?ㅽ겕媛 ?녿뒗媛"瑜?紐⑸줉???듯븳??
 243:                 item.put("monitoredBy", monitoredBy(inst, region));
 244:                 ec2s.add(item);
 245:             }
 246:         } catch (Exception e) {
 247:             log.error("Error get EC2 on region {}: {}", region, e.getMessage());
 248:         }
 249:         return ec2s;
 250:     }

 284:     @Override
 285:     public Map<String, Object> summaryInstances(List<String> regions) {
 286:         List<String> listRegion = regions != null ? regions : getRegions();
 287:         List<Callable<RegionInstances>> tasks = new ArrayList<>();
 288:         for (String region : listRegion) {
 289:             tasks.add(() -> instancesWithStatus(region));
 290:         }
 291: 
 292:         Map<String, Integer> ec2ByRegion = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 293:         Map<String, Integer> ec2ByType = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 294:         Map<String, Integer> ec2ByState = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 295:         Map<String, Map<String, Integer>> ec2ByRegionAndStatus = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 296: 
 297:         // AWS ?몄텧? 蹂묐젹, 吏묎퀎???쒖꽌 蹂댁〈 ?⑥씪 ?ㅻ젅??硫깅벑)濡??섑뻾.
 298:         for (RegionInstances ri : runParallel(tasks)) {
 299:             if (ri == null) {
 300:                 continue;
 301:             }
 302:             ec2ByRegion.merge(ri.region(), ri.instances().size(), Integer::sum);
 303:             ec2ByRegionAndStatus.put(ri.region(), new LinkedHashMap<>());
 304:             for (Instance inst : ri.instances()) {
 305:                 ec2ByType.merge(inst.instanceTypeAsString(), 1, Integer::sum);
 306:                 ec2ByState.merge(inst.state() != null ? inst.state().nameAsString() : null, 1, Integer::sum);
 307:                 String status = ri.statusMap().get(inst.instanceId());
 308:                 ec2ByRegionAndStatus.get(ri.region()).merge(status, 1, Integer::sum);
 309:             }
 310:         }
 311: 
 312:         Map<String, Object> items = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 313:         items.put("ec2ByRegion", ec2ByRegion);
 314:         items.put("ec2ByType", ec2ByType);
 315:         items.put("ec2ByState", ec2ByState);
 316:         items.put("ec2ByRegionAndStatus", ec2ByRegionAndStatus);
 317:         return Map.of("items", items);
 318:     }

 320:     /** 臾닿낵湲?媛쒖슂: EC2 ?몃깽?좊━? ?곹깭 ?먭?留??ъ슜?쒕떎. */
 321:     @Override
 322:     public Map<String, Object> overview(List<String> regions, TimeRange timeRange) {
 323:         List<String> targets = regions != null ? regions : getRegions();
 324:         List<Callable<OverviewRegion>> tasks = targets.stream()
 325:                 .map(region -> (Callable<OverviewRegion>) () -> overviewForRegion(region)).toList();
 326:         List<OverviewRegion> parts = runParallel(tasks);
 327:         List<Map<String, Object>> collection = new ArrayList<>();
 328:         List<Map<String, Object>> risks = new ArrayList<>();
 329:         int healthy=0, warning=0, critical=0, unobserved=0, stopped=0, total=0;
 330:         for (OverviewRegion p : parts) {
 331:             if (p == null) continue;
 332:             collection.add(p.collection()); risks.addAll(p.risks());
 333:             total += p.total; healthy += p.healthy; warning += p.warning; critical += p.critical; unobserved += p.unobserved; stopped += p.stopped;
 334:         }
 335:         Map<String,Integer> coverage = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 336:         coverage.put("zabbix-agent", 0); coverage.put("cloudwatch", 0);
 337:         coverage.put("unavailable", 0); coverage.put("unmonitored", 0);
 338:         for (OverviewRegion p : parts) {

 361:     private OverviewRegion overviewForRegion(String region) {
 362:         Instant collected = Instant.now();
 363:         List<Instance> instances;
 364:         Map<String,String> statuses;
 365:         try {
 366:             instances = aws.describeInstances(region);
 367:             statuses = instanceStatuses(region, instances.stream().map(Instance::instanceId).toList());
 368:         } catch (Exception e) {
 369:             return OverviewRegion.unavailable(region, "EC2_UNAVAILABLE");
 370:         }
 371:         List<Map<String,Object>> risks=new ArrayList<>();
 372:         Map<String,Integer> coverage=new LinkedHashMap<>();
 373:         int healthy=0,warning=0,critical=0,unobserved=0,stopped=0;
 374:         for (Instance i : instances) {
 375:             coverage.merge(monitoredBy(i, region), 1, Integer::sum);
 376:             String state=i.state()==null ? "unknown" : i.state().nameAsString(); String check=statuses.get(i.instanceId());
 377:             // ?먯젙 洹쒖튃? ?ㅻ깄???섏쭛(CloudSnapshotService)怨?怨듭쑀?쒕떎 ??蹂듭젣 湲덉?.
 378:             String severity = Ec2StatusRules.classify(state, check);
 379:             switch (severity) {
 380:                 case Ec2StatusRules.SEVERITY_STOPPED -> stopped++;
 381:                 case Ec2StatusRules.SEVERITY_CRITICAL -> critical++;
 382:                 case Ec2StatusRules.SEVERITY_WARNING -> warning++;
 383:                 case Ec2StatusRules.SEVERITY_UNOBSERVED -> unobserved++;
 384:                 default -> healthy++;
 385:             }
 386:             if (!"healthy".equals(severity)) { Map<String,Object> x=overviewInstance(i,region,check,severity); x.put("rank", "critical".equals(severity)?4:"warning".equals(severity)?3:"unobserved".equals(severity)?2:1); risks.add(x); }
 387:         }
 388:         Map<String,Object> ec2 = collectionState("ok", collected.toString(), null);
 389:         Map<String,Object> disabled = collectionState("disabled", null, "ZERO_COST_MODE");
 390:         return new OverviewRegion(Map.of("region",region,"ec2",ec2,"cloudWatch",disabled,"alarms",disabled), instances.size(),healthy,warning,critical,unobserved,stopped,risks,coverage);

 456:     public Map<String, Object> getVpcDetail(String vpcId, String region) {

 461:             List<RouteTable> routeTables = aws.describeRouteTablesByVpc(region, vpcId);

 463:             List<Instance> instances = aws.describeInstancesByVpc(region, vpcId);
 464:

 466:             Map<String, Object> result = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 467:             result.put("vpc", vpcDetail(vpc, region));
 468:             result.put("subnets", subnets.stream().map(this::subnetDetail).toList());
 469:             result.put("routeTables", routeTables.stream().map(this::routeTableDetail).toList());
 470:             result.put("internetGateways", aws.describeInternetGatewaysByVpc(region, vpcId).stream()
 471:                     .map(g -> Map.of("internetGatewayId", g.internetGatewayId(), "state", g.attachments().stream()
 472:                             .map(a -> a.stateAsString()).findFirst().orElse("unknown"))).toList());

 474:                 Map<String, Object> x = new LinkedHashMap<>(); x.put("natGatewayId", n.natGatewayId()); x.put("state", n.stateAsString());
 475:                 x.put("subnetId", n.subnetId()); x.put("connectivityType", n.connectivityTypeAsString()); return x;
 476:             }).toList());
 477:             result.put("securityGroups", groups.stream().map(this::securityGroupDetail).toList());
 478:             result.put("instances", instances.stream().map(this::instanceDetail).toList());
 479:             result.put("exposureFindings", findings);
 480:             result.put("collection", Map.of("state", "ok", "region", region, "cloudWatchUsed", false));
 481:             return result;
 482:         } catch (Ec2Exception e) {
 483:             if (awsNotFoundError("vpc", e.awsErrorDetails() == null ? null : e.awsErrorDetails().errorCode())
 484:                     == ErrorCode.VPC_NOT_FOUND) {
 485:                 throw new ApiError(ErrorCode.VPC_NOT_FOUND);
 486:             }

 491:     /**
 492:      * AWS SDK??resource-specific not-found留??덉젙??DMOVE API ?ㅻ쪟濡?諛붽씔??
 493:      *
 494:      * <p>{@code .Malformed}??not-found濡?痍④툒?쒕떎 ??AWS???뺤떇???닿툔??ID??NotFound ???
 495:      * Malformed瑜??섏??붾뜲(?고????ㅼ륫 2026-08-24: {@code Invalid id} ??500 ?좎텧), ?ъ슜??
 496:      * ?낆옣?먯꽑 ????"洹몃윴 由ъ냼???놁쓬"?대떎. 援щ텇??遊ㅼ옄 ?덈궡媛 ?щ씪吏吏 ?딅뒗??
 497:      */
 498:     static ErrorCode awsNotFoundError(String resource, String awsErrorCode) {
 499:         if (awsErrorCode == null || !(awsErrorCode.endsWith(".NotFound") || awsErrorCode.endsWith(".Malformed"))) {
 500:             return null;
 501:         }
 502:         return "instance".equals(resource) && awsErrorCode.contains("Instance")
 503:                 ? ErrorCode.INSTANCE_NOT_FOUND
 504:                 : "vpc".equals(resource) && awsErrorCode.contains("Vpc")
 505:                         ? ErrorCode.VPC_NOT_FOUND : null;
 506:     }

 508:     @Override
 509:     public Map<String, Object> getCloudTrailEvents(String region, String resourceId, String eventName, int maxResults) {
 510:         try {
 511:             Instant end = Instant.now();
 512:             List<Map<String, Object>> items = aws.lookupEvents(region, resourceId, eventName, maxResults,
 513:                     end.minus(java.time.Duration.ofDays(90)), end).stream().map(event -> {
 514:                 Map<String, Object> x = new LinkedHashMap<>(); x.put("eventId", event.eventId()); x.put("eventName", event.eventName());
 515:                 x.put("eventTime", event.eventTime() == null ? null : event.eventTime().toString()); x.put("username", event.username());
 516:                 x.put("resources", event.resources().stream().map(resource -> Map.of("type", resource.resourceType(), "name", resource.resourceName())).toList());
 517:                 x.put("readOnly", event.readOnly()); x.put("source", event.eventSource()); return x;
 518:             }).toList();
 519:             return Map.of("items", items, "collection", Map.of("state", "ok", "region", region,
 520:                     "retentionDays", 90, "nextTokenAvailable", false));
 521:         } catch (Exception e) {
 522:             log.warn("CloudTrail event history unavailable in {}: {}", region, e.getMessage());
 523:             return Map.of("items", List.of(), "collection", Map.of("state", "unavailable", "region", region,
 524:                     "reason", "CLOUDTRAIL_EVENT_HISTORY_UNAVAILABLE", "retentionDays", 90));
 525:         }
 526:     }

 531:         x.put("state", i.state() == null ? null : i.state().nameAsString()); x.put("instanceType", i.instanceTypeAsString());
 532:         x.put("availabilityZone", i.placement() == null ? null : i.placement().availabilityZone()); x.put("vpcId", i.vpcId());
 533:         x.put("subnetId", i.subnetId()); x.put("privateIp", i.privateIpAddress()); x.put("publicIp", i.publicIpAddress());
 534:         return x;

 537:     private Map<String, Object> statusChecks(InstanceStatus status) {
 538:         Map<String, Object> x = new LinkedHashMap<>(); x.put("overall", status == null ? "unobserved" : instanceStatusesFrom(status));
 539:         x.put("system", status == null || status.systemStatus() == null ? "unobserved" : status.systemStatus().statusAsString());
 540:         x.put("instance", status == null || status.instanceStatus() == null ? "unobserved" : status.instanceStatus().statusAsString()); return x;
 541:     }

 544:         return Ec2StatusRules.overallStatus(s);
 545:     }

 550:             x.put("description", e.description()); x.put("notBefore", e.notBefore() == null ? null : e.notBefore().toString());
 551:             x.put("notAfter", e.notAfter() == null ? null : e.notAfter().toString()); return x; }).toList();
 552:     }

 561:     private Map<String, Object> permissionDetail(IpPermission p) { Map<String,Object> x=new LinkedHashMap<>();x.put("protocol",p.ipProtocol());x.put("fromPort",p.fromPort());x.put("toPort",p.toPort());x.put("ipv4Cidrs",p.ipRanges().stream().map(r->r.cidrIp()).toList());x.put("ipv6Cidrs",p.ipv6Ranges().stream().map(r->r.cidrIpv6()).toList());return x; }
 562: 
 563:     private Map<String, String> instanceStatuses(String region, List<String> instanceIds) {
 564:         Map<String, String> statusMap = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 565:         if (instanceIds.isEmpty()) {
 566:             return statusMap;
 567:         }
 568:         List<InstanceStatus> statuses = aws.describeInstanceStatuses(region, instanceIds);
 569:         for (InstanceStatus s : statuses) {
 570:             int passed = 0;
 571:             if (s.systemStatus() != null && "ok".equals(s.systemStatus().statusAsString())) {
 572:                 passed++;
 573:             }
 574:             if (s.instanceStatus() != null && "ok".equals(s.instanceStatus().statusAsString())) {
 575:                 passed++;
 576:             }
 577:             String check = passed == 2 ? "2/2 checks passed" : passed == 1 ? "1/2 checks failed" : "0/2 checks failed";
 578:             statusMap.put(s.instanceId(), check);
 579:         }
 580:         return statusMap;
 581:     }
 582: 
 583:     private Map<String, String> vpcNameMap(String region, List<String> vpcIds) {
 584:         Map<String, String> map = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 585:         if (vpcIds.isEmpty()) {
 586:             return map;
 587:         }
 588:         for (Vpc vpc : aws.describeVpcs(region, vpcIds)) {
 589:             String name = vpc.tags().stream().filter(t -> "Name".equals(t.key()))
 590:                     .map(t -> t.value()).findFirst().orElse(vpc.vpcId());
 591:             map.put(vpc.vpcId(), name);
 592:         }
 593:         return map;
 594:     }
 595: 
 596:     private String str(Object o) {
 597:         return o != null ? o.toString() : null;
 598:     }

  73:     public Map<String, Object> metrics(String instanceId, String region, long fromEpoch, long toEpoch) {
  74:         Instant now = Instant.now();
  75:         Instant to = toEpoch > 0 ? Instant.ofEpochSecond(toEpoch) : now;
  76:         Instant from = fromEpoch > 0 ? Instant.ofEpochSecond(fromEpoch) : to.minus(Duration.ofDays(1));
  77: 
  78:         // ?? 1) Zabbix agent 寃쎈줈 ??留ㅼ묶 + ?덉쭏 寃뚯씠???듦낵 ??CW瑜?遺瑜댁? ?딅뒗???꾨━?곗뼱 ?덉빟)
  79:         Optional<Ec2ZabbixMatcher.Match> match = findMatch(instanceId, region);
  80:         if (match.isPresent()) {
  81:             Map<String, Object> agent = tryAgentSeries(match.get(), from, to, now);
  82:             if (agent != null) {
  83:                 return agent;
  84:             }
  85:         }
  86: 
  87:         // ?? 2) CloudWatch 寃쎈줈(?꾪뻾) ??留ㅼ묶?먮뒗???ш린濡??붾떎硫?紐낆떆??fallback?대떎
  88:         Map<String, Object> result = cloudWatchSeries(instanceId, region, from, to, now);
  89:         @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
  90:         Map<String, Object> collection = (Map<String, Object>) result.get("collection");
  91:         collection.put("source", "cloudwatch");
  92:         collection.put("fallbackUsed", match.isPresent());
  93:         match.ifPresent(m -> collection.put("matchBasis", m.basis()));
  94:         return result;
  95:     }
  96: 
  97:     private Optional<Ec2ZabbixMatcher.Match> findMatch(String instanceId, String region) {
  98:         try {
  99:             Instance instance = aws.findInstance(region, instanceId);
 100:             return matcher.matchFor(instance);
 101:         } catch (Exception e) {
 102:             log.debug("ec2 lookup for matching failed ({}): {}", instanceId, e.getMessage());
 103:             return Optional.empty();
 104:         }
 105:     }
 106: 
 107:     /**
 108:      * agent ?쒓퀎????CPU util(%)쨌硫붾え由?pused(%). 1李?踰붿쐞: ?⑥씪 ?쒕━利??뺤젙 吏?쒕쭔
 109:      * (?붿뒪???ъ슜瑜졖룸꽕?몄썙?щ뒗 ?뚰떚???명꽣?섏씠???ㅼ감?먯씠??2李????ㅺ퀎 짠4).
 110:      *
 111:      * @return ?덉쭏 寃뚯씠??????議댁옱 + ?좎꽑) 誘몃떖?대㈃ null ???몄텧遺媛 CW fallback.
 112:      */
 113:     private Map<String, Object> tryAgentSeries(Ec2ZabbixMatcher.Match match, Instant from, Instant to, Instant now) {
 114:         try {
 115:             EntityRef entity = EntityRef.ofDefault(match.hostid());
 116:             TimeRange range = new TimeRange(from.getEpochSecond(), to.getEpochSecond());
 117:             // ?뵶 ?쒖뒪??而⑦뀓?ㅽ듃 ???대씪?곕뱶 ?붾㈃? awsResources.view濡?寃뚯씠?몃릺硫? ?ㅻ깄??異뺢낵 ?숈씪?섍쾶
 118:             //    ?쒖뒪???섏쭛 ?곗씠?곕? ?뚮퉬?쒕떎(?ъ슜?먮퀎 Zabbix ?몄뀡???쒖슦硫?罹먯떆쨌沅뚰븳???쏀엺????짠8).
 119:             List<Series> cpu = metricPort.querySeries(SRC, CallerContext.system(),
 120:                     SeriesQuery.of(entity, MetricRef.of(MetricDomain.CPU, "util"), range, Rollup.RAW));
 121:             List<Series> memory = metricPort.querySeries(SRC, CallerContext.system(),
 122:                     SeriesQuery.of(entity, MetricRef.of(MetricDomain.MEMORY, "pused"), range, Rollup.RAW));
 123: 
 124:             long latestEpoch = Math.max(latestEpoch(cpu), latestEpoch(memory));
 125:             boolean fresh = latestEpoch > 0
 126:                     && Instant.ofEpochSecond(latestEpoch).isAfter(now.minus(AGENT_FRESHNESS));
 127:             if (seriesEmpty(cpu) || seriesEmpty(memory) || !fresh) {
 128:                 log.debug("agent series quality gate failed for host {} (fresh={})", match.hostid(), fresh);
 129:                 return null;
 130:             }
 131: 
 132:             Map<String, Object> series = emptySeries();
 133:             series.put("cpu", toPoints(cpu));
 134:             series.put("memoryUsage", toPoints(memory));
 135:             Map<String, Object> collection = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 136:             collection.put("state", "ok");
 137:             collection.put("reason", null);
 138:             collection.put("source", "zabbix-agent");
 139:             collection.put("fallbackUsed", false);
 140:             collection.put("matchBasis", match.basis());
 141:             collection.put("zabbixHostid", match.hostid());
 142:             collection.put("zabbixHostName", match.hostName());
 143:             collection.put("observedAt", Instant.ofEpochSecond(latestEpoch).toString());
 144:             return Map.of("items", series, "collection", collection);
 145:         } catch (Exception e) {
 146:             // Zabbix ?μ븷??CW fallback ?ъ쑀????二쎌? ?딅뒗??
 147:             log.warn("agent series fetch failed for host {}: {}", match.hostid(), e.getMessage());
 148:             return null;
 149:         }
 150:     }
 151: 
 152:     private Map<String, Object> cloudWatchSeries(String instanceId, String region, Instant from, Instant to,
 153:             Instant now) {
 154:         if (availability.isDenied(region)) {
 155:             return degraded("unavailable", "CLOUDWATCH_ACCESS_DENIED");
 156:         }
 157:         if (availability.budgetExhausted(CW_METRICS.size())) {
 158:             log.warn("cloud metrics budget exhausted: used={} cap={}", availability.usedThisMonth(),
 159:                     availability.cap());
 160:             return degraded("disabled", "BUDGET_EXHAUSTED");
 161:         }
 162:         long used = availability.usedThisMonth();
 163:         int period = resolvePeriodSeconds(from, to, now);
 164:         Map<String, Object> series = emptySeries();
 165:         int calls = 0;
 166:         try {
 167:             for (MetricSpec metric : CW_METRICS) {
 168:                 calls++;
 169:                 List<Datapoint> points = aws.getMetricStatistics(region, NAMESPACE, metric.metricName(),
 170:                         "InstanceId", instanceId, from, to, period, metric.statistic());
 171:                 series.put(metric.key(), points.stream()
 172:                         // 臾몄꽌 紐낆떆: ?곗씠?고룷?명듃???쒓컙?쒖씠 ?꾨땲?????ш린???뺣젹??FE 遺?댁쓣 ?놁븻??
 173:                         .sorted(Comparator.comparing(Datapoint::timestamp))
 174:                         .map(p -> Map.of("time", p.timestamp().toString(),
 175:                                 "value", statisticValue(p, metric.statistic())))
 176:                         .toList());
 177:             }
 178:         } catch (AwsServiceException e) {
 179:             recordCalls(calls);
 180:             if (isAccessDenied(e)) {
 181:                 // ???곕뒗 怨좉컼? ?뺤긽 ?곹깭 ??由ъ쟾 ?⑥쐞 TTL 罹먯떆濡??ъ떆?꾨? 硫덉텛怨?議곗슜??媛뺣벑?쒕떎.
 182:                 availability.markDenied(region);
 183:                 log.info("CloudWatch access denied in {} ??metrics degraded: {}", region, e.getMessage());
 184:                 return degraded("unavailable", "CLOUDWATCH_ACCESS_DENIED");
 185:             }
 186:             log.warn("CloudWatch metrics unavailable in {}: {}", region, e.getMessage());
 187:             return degraded("unavailable", "CLOUDWATCH_UNAVAILABLE");
 188:         }
 189:         recordCalls(calls);
 190:         Map<String, Object> collection = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 191:         collection.put("state", "ok");
 192:         collection.put("reason", null);
 193:         collection.put("periodSeconds", period);
 194:         collection.put("budget", Map.of("used", used + calls, "cap", availability.cap()));
 195:         Map<String, Object> result = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 196:         result.put("items", series);
 197:         result.put("collection", collection);
 198:         return result;
 199:     }
 200: 
 201:     /**
 202:      * Period ?곗젙 ??議고쉶 湲곌컙怨?CloudWatch retention 洹쒖튃???④퍡 留뚯”?쒗궓??
 203:      * 洹쒖튃(怨듭떇 臾몄꽌): ?쒖옉??15???댁쟾?대㈃ 300s 諛곗닔, 63???댁쟾?대㈃ 3600s 諛곗닔. ?ш린??     * "?몄텧??理쒕? 1,440 ?ъ씤??瑜?base 諛곗닔 ?뺣?濡?吏?⑤떎.
 204:      */
 205:     static int resolvePeriodSeconds(Instant from, Instant to, Instant now) {
 206:         int base = from.isAfter(now.minus(Duration.ofDays(63))) ? 300 : 3600;
 207:         long rangeSeconds = Math.max(1, Duration.between(from, to).getSeconds());
 208:         long minPeriod = (long) Math.ceil(rangeSeconds / (double) MAX_DATAPOINTS);
 209:         long multiples = Math.max(1, (long) Math.ceil(minPeriod / (double) base));
 210:         return (int) (multiples * base);
 211:     }
 212: 
 213:     private void recordCalls(int calls) {
 214:         if (calls > 0) {
 215:             budgetRepository.addCalls(LocalDate.now().format(DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyy-MM")), calls);
 216:         }
 217:     }
 218: 
 219:     private static boolean isAccessDenied(AwsServiceException e) {
 220:         String code = e.awsErrorDetails() == null ? null : e.awsErrorDetails().errorCode();
 221:         return e.statusCode() == 403
 222:                 || "AccessDenied".equals(code) || "AccessDeniedException".equals(code)
 223:                 || "UnauthorizedOperation".equals(code);
 224:     }
 225: 
 226:     private static double statisticValue(Datapoint p, Statistic statistic) {
 227:         Double value = statistic == Statistic.AVERAGE ? p.average()
 228:                 : statistic == Statistic.MAXIMUM ? p.maximum()
 229:                         : statistic == Statistic.MINIMUM ? p.minimum() : p.sum();
 230:         return value == null ? 0d : value;
 231:     }
 232: 
 233:     private static boolean seriesEmpty(List<Series> series) {
 234:         return series.isEmpty() || series.get(0).points().isEmpty();
 235:     }
 236: 
 237:     private static long latestEpoch(List<Series> series) {
 238:         return series.stream().flatMap(s -> s.points().stream())
 239:                 .mapToLong(p -> p.epoch()).max().orElse(0L);
 240:     }
 241: 
 242:     private static List<Map<String, Object>> toPoints(List<Series> series) {
 243:         return series.get(0).points().stream()
 244:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparingLong(p -> p.epoch()))
 245:                 .map(p -> Map.<String, Object>of("time", Instant.ofEpochSecond(p.epoch()).toString(),
 246:                         "value", p.value()))
 247:                 .toList();
 248:     }
 249: 
 250:     private static Map<String, Object> emptySeries() {
 251:         Map<String, Object> series = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 252:         for (String key : ALL_KEYS) {
 253:             series.put(key, List.of());
 254:         }
 255:         return series;
 256:     }

 258:     /** 媛뺣벑 ?묐떟 ??紐⑤뱺 吏???ㅻ? 鍮?諛곗뿴濡??좎???FE媛 ??議댁옱 ?щ?濡?遺꾧린?섏? ?딄쾶 ?쒕떎. */
 259:     private static Map<String, Object> degraded(String state, String reason) {
 260:         Map<String, Object> collection = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 261:         collection.put("state", state);
 262:         collection.put("reason", reason);
 263:         Map<String, Object> result = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 264:         result.put("items", emptySeries());
 265:         result.put("collection", collection);
 266:         return result;
 267:     }

 270:     }

### src/main/java/com/dmove/moveseye/aws/AwsServiceImpl.java
   1: package com.dmove.moveseye.aws;
   2: 
   3: import com.dmove.moveseye.common.exception.ApiError;
   4: import com.dmove.moveseye.common.exception.ErrorCode;
   5: import com.dmove.moveseye.aws.client.AwsReadOnlyGateway;
   6: import com.dmove.moveseye.aws.dto.CloudOverviewQueryDto.TimeRange;

   8: import java.util.ArrayList;
   9: import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
  10: import java.util.LinkedHashSet;
  11: import java.util.List;
  12: import java.util.Map;
  13: import java.util.Set;

  15: import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;
  16: import java.util.concurrent.Executors;
  17: import java.util.concurrent.Future;
  18: import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;

  31: 
  32: /** NestJS src/aws/aws.service.ts ?ы똿 (AWS SDK v2). */
  33: @Slf4j
  34: @Service
  35: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  36: public class AwsServiceImpl implements AwsService {
  37: 
  38:     /** 由ъ쟾 ?ъ븘??吏??蹂묐젹 ?몄텧 ?곹븳(由ъ쟾??留롮븘???ㅻ젅????＜ 諛⑹?). */
  39:     private static final int MAX_PARALLELISM = 16;
  40: 
  41:     private final AwsReadOnlyGateway aws;
  42:     private final CloudSnapshotQueryService snapshotQuery;
  43:     private final Ec2ZabbixMatcher matcher;
  44:     private final CloudWatchAvailability cloudWatchAvailability;
  45: 
  46:     /**
  47:      * 紐⑤땲?곕쭅 而ㅻ쾭由ъ? ?먯젙(?뚯뒪 留ㅽ듃由?뒪, devdesign_ec2-monitoring-sources.md 짠4 ??4媛?:
  48:      * zabbix-agent(留ㅼ묶+?좎꽑) / cloudwatch(CW 媛?? / unavailable(沅뚰븳쨌?덉궛쨌?섏쭛 ?ㅽ뙣) /
  49:      * unmonitored(以묒? ?????꾩옱 吏?쒓? ?대뵒?먮룄 ?놁쓬). AWS 異붽? ?몄텧 0(留ㅼ쿂 罹먯떆 + CW 媛?⑹꽦 罹먯떆留?.
  50:      */
  51:     private String monitoredBy(Instance instance, String region) {
  52:         java.util.Optional<Ec2ZabbixMatcher.Match> match = matcher.matchFor(instance);
  53:         if (match.isPresent() && matcher.isFresh(match.get(), java.time.Duration.ofMinutes(30))) {
  54:             return "zabbix-agent";
  55:         }
  56:         String state = instance.state() == null ? "unknown" : instance.state().nameAsString();
  57:         if ("stopped".equals(state)) {
  58:             return "unmonitored";
  59:         }
  60:         if ("ok".equals(cloudWatchAvailability.availability(region))) {
  61:             return "cloudwatch";
  62:         }
  63:         return "unavailable";
  64:     }
  65: 
  66:     /**
  67:      * ?щ윭 ?묒뾽???쒗븳???ㅻ젅?쒗??먯꽌 蹂묐젹 ?ㅽ뻾?섍퀬 ?쒖텧 ?쒖꽌?濡?寃곌낵瑜?紐⑥???
  68:      * 由ъ쟾 ?ъ븘??由ъ쟾??AWS ?몄텧 ?섏떗 ?????쒖감?믩퀝?щ줈 諛붽퓭 踰쎌떆怨??쒓컙???⑷퀎?먯꽌 理쒕? 1嫄댁쑝濡??⑥텞.
  69:      * 媛쒕퀎 ?묒뾽 ?ㅽ뙣??null濡??섏쭛(?몄텧遺?먯꽌 skip). ?묒뾽 肄쒕갚? ?먯껜?곸쑝濡??덉쇅瑜?泥섎━??遺遺꾧컪??諛섑솚?섎뒗 寃껋쓣 沅뚯옣.
  70:      */
  71:     private <T> List<T> runParallel(List<? extends Callable<T>> tasks) {
  72:         if (tasks.isEmpty()) {
  73:             return List.of();
  74:         }
  75:         if (tasks.size() == 1) {
  76:             try {
  77:                 return new ArrayList<>(List.of(tasks.get(0).call()));
  78:             } catch (Exception e) {
  79:                 log.error("parallel task failed: {}", e.getMessage());
  80:                 List<T> single = new ArrayList<>();
  81:                 single.add(null);
  82:                 return single;
  83:             }
  84:         }
  85:         ExecutorService pool = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(Math.min(tasks.size(), MAX_PARALLELISM));
  86:         try {
  87:             List<Future<T>> futures = new ArrayList<>();
  88:             for (Callable<T> task : tasks) {
  89:                 futures.add(pool.submit(task));
  90:             }
  91:             List<T> results = new ArrayList<>();
  92:             for (Future<T> f : futures) {
  93:                 try {
  94:                     results.add(f.get());
  95:                 } catch (Exception e) {
  96:                     log.error("parallel task failed: {}", e.getMessage());
  97:                     results.add(null);
  98:                 }
  99:             }
 100:             return results;
 101:         } finally {
 102:             pool.shutdown();
 103:         }
 104:     }
 105: 
 106:     @Override
 107:     public List<String> getRegions() {
 108:         return aws.describeRegions().stream()
 109:                 .filter(r -> "opt-in-not-required".equals(r.optInStatus()))
 110:                 .map(software.amazon.awssdk.services.ec2.model.Region::regionName)
 111:                 .toList();
 112:     }
 113: 
 114:     @Override
 115:     public Map<String, Object> getVpcs(String keyword, List<String> regions) {
 116:         List<String> listRegion = regions != null ? regions : getRegions();
 117:         List<Callable<List<Map<String, Object>>>> tasks = new ArrayList<>();
 118:         for (String region : listRegion) {
 119:             tasks.add(() -> vpcsForRegion(region, keyword));
 120:         }
 121:         List<Map<String, Object>> vpcs = new ArrayList<>();
 122:         for (List<Map<String, Object>> part : runParallel(tasks)) {
 123:             if (part != null) {
 124:                 vpcs.addAll(part);
 125:             }
 126:         }
 127:         return Map.of("items", vpcs);
 128:     }
 129: 
 130:     /** ?⑥씪 由ъ쟾??VPC 紐⑸줉. AWS ?몄텧 ?ㅽ뙣??濡쒓렇 ??鍮?紐⑸줉(?먮낯??region 猷⑦봽 ??skip 嫄곕룞 ?좎?). */
 131:     private List<Map<String, Object>> vpcsForRegion(String region, String keyword) {
 132:         List<Map<String, Object>> vpcs = new ArrayList<>();
 133:         try {
 134:             List<Vpc> vpcList = aws.describeVpcs(region);
 135:             if (vpcList.isEmpty()) {
 136:                 return vpcs;
 137:             }
 138:             List<Subnet> subnets = aws.describeSubnets(region);
 139:             List<Instance> instances = aws.describeInstances(region);
 140: 
 141:             // vpcId ??subnet??mapPublicIpOnLaunch 紐⑸줉
 142:             Map<String, List<Boolean>> subnetMap = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 143:             for (Subnet s : subnets) {
 144:                 if (s.vpcId() != null) {
 145:                     subnetMap.computeIfAbsent(s.vpcId(), k -> new ArrayList<>())
 146:                             .add(Boolean.TRUE.equals(s.mapPublicIpOnLaunch()));
 147:                 }
 148:             }
 149:             // vpcId ??instance ??
 150:             Map<String, Integer> instanceCount = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 151:             for (Instance i : instances) {
 152:                 if (i.vpcId() != null) {
 153:                     instanceCount.merge(i.vpcId(), 1, Integer::sum);
 154:                 }
 155:             }
 156: 
 157:             for (Vpc vpc : vpcList) {
 158:                 String vpcName = vpc.tags().stream().filter(t -> "Name".equals(t.key()))
 159:                         .map(t -> t.value()).findFirst().orElse("-");
 160:                 if (keyword != null && !vpcName.toLowerCase().contains(keyword.toLowerCase())) {
 161:                     continue;
 162:                 }
 163:                 List<Boolean> subList = subnetMap.get(vpc.vpcId());
 164:                 String blockPublicAccess = (subList != null && subList.stream().allMatch(b -> !b)) ? "Off" : "On";
 165: 
 166:                 Map<String, Object> item = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 167:                 item.put("vpcId", vpc.vpcId());
 168:                 item.put("name", vpcName);
 169:                 item.put("ownerId", vpc.ownerId());
 170:                 item.put("region", region);
 171:                 item.put("state", vpc.stateAsString());
 172:                 item.put("ec2Count", instanceCount.getOrDefault(vpc.vpcId(), 0));
 173:                 item.put("ipv4Cidr", vpc.cidrBlock());
 174:                 item.put("ipv6Cidr", vpc.ipv6CidrBlockAssociationSet().stream()
 175:                         .map(x -> x.ipv6CidrBlock()).toList());
 176:                 item.put("tenancy", vpc.instanceTenancyAsString());
 177:                 item.put("blockPublicAccess", blockPublicAccess);
 178:                 vpcs.add(item);
 179:             }
 180:         } catch (Exception e) {
 181:             log.error("Error get VPC on region {}: {}", region, e.getMessage());
 182:         }
 183:         return vpcs;
 184:     }

 186:     @Override
 187:     public Map<String, Object> listInstancesDetailed(List<String> regions, List<String> vpcIds) {
 188:         List<String> listRegion = regions != null ? regions : getRegions();
 189:         List<Callable<List<Map<String, Object>>>> tasks = new ArrayList<>();
 190:         for (String region : listRegion) {
 191:             tasks.add(() -> instancesForRegion(region, vpcIds));
 192:         }
 193:         List<Map<String, Object>> ec2s = new ArrayList<>();
 194:         for (List<Map<String, Object>> part : runParallel(tasks)) {
 195:             if (part != null) {
 196:                 ec2s.addAll(part);
 197:             }
 198:         }
 199:         return Map.of("items", ec2s);
 200:     }
 201: 
 202:     /** ?⑥씪 由ъ쟾??EC2 ?곸꽭 紐⑸줉. */
 203:     private List<Map<String, Object>> instancesForRegion(String region, List<String> vpcIds) {
 204:         List<Map<String, Object>> ec2s = new ArrayList<>();
 205:         try {
 206:             List<Instance> instances = aws.describeInstances(region);
 207:             if (instances.isEmpty()) {
 208:                 return ec2s;
 209:             }
 210:             List<String> instanceIds = instances.stream().map(Instance::instanceId)
 211:                     .filter(java.util.Objects::nonNull).toList();
 212:             Set<String> vpcIdSet = new LinkedHashSet<>();
 213:             instances.forEach(i -> {
 214:                 if (i.vpcId() != null) {
 215:                     vpcIdSet.add(i.vpcId());
 216:                 }
 217:             });
 218: 
 219:             Map<String, String> statusMap = instanceStatuses(region, instanceIds);
 220:             Map<String, String> vpcNameMap = vpcNameMap(region, new ArrayList<>(vpcIdSet));
 221: 
 222:             for (Instance inst : instances) {
 223:                 if (vpcIds != null && (inst.vpcId() == null || !vpcIds.contains(inst.vpcId()))) {
 224:                     continue;
 225:                 }
 226:                 String vpcId = inst.vpcId() != null ? inst.vpcId() : "-";
 227:                 String name = inst.tags().stream().filter(t -> "Name".equals(t.key()))
 228:                         .map(t -> t.value()).findFirst().orElse("-");
 229: 
 230:                 Map<String, Object> item = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 231:                 item.put("instanceName", name);
 232:                 item.put("instanceId", inst.instanceId());
 233:                 item.put("instanceState", inst.state() != null ? inst.state().nameAsString() : "-");
 234:                 item.put("instanceType", inst.instanceTypeAsString() != null ? inst.instanceTypeAsString() : "-");
 235:                 item.put("statusCheck", statusMap.getOrDefault(inst.instanceId(), "-"));
 236:                 item.put("region", region);
 237:                 item.put("availabilityZone", inst.placement() != null ? inst.placement().availabilityZone() : "-");
 238:                 item.put("vpcName", vpcNameMap.getOrDefault(vpcId, vpcId));
 239:                 item.put("vpcId", vpcId);
 240:                 item.put("securityGroupName", inst.securityGroups().stream()
 241:                         .map(g -> g.groupName()).reduce((a, b) -> a + ", " + b).orElse("-"));
 242:                 // ?뚯뒪 留ㅽ듃由?뒪 而ㅻ쾭由ъ?(4媛? ??"?????몄뒪?댁뒪??硫붾え由?룸뵒?ㅽ겕媛 ?녿뒗媛"瑜?紐⑸줉???듯븳??
 243:                 item.put("monitoredBy", monitoredBy(inst, region));
 244:                 ec2s.add(item);
 245:             }
 246:         } catch (Exception e) {
 247:             log.error("Error get EC2 on region {}: {}", region, e.getMessage());
 248:         }
 249:         return ec2s;
 250:     }

 265:     /** 蹂묐젹 ?ъ븘?껋슜: ?⑥씪 由ъ쟾???몄뒪?댁뒪 + ?곹깭留? ?몄뒪?댁뒪 ?녾굅???ㅽ뙣 ??null. */
 266:     private record RegionInstances(String region, List<Instance> instances, Map<String, String> statusMap) {

 268: 
 269:     private RegionInstances instancesWithStatus(String region) {
 270:         try {
 271:             List<Instance> instances = aws.describeInstances(region);
 272:             if (instances.isEmpty()) {
 273:                 return null;
 274:             }
 275:             List<String> instanceIds = instances.stream().map(Instance::instanceId)
 276:                     .filter(java.util.Objects::nonNull).toList();
 277:             return new RegionInstances(region, instances, instanceStatuses(region, instanceIds));
 278:         } catch (Exception e) {
 279:             log.error("Error get EC2 on region {}: {}", region, e.getMessage());
 280:             return null;

 284:     @Override
 285:     public Map<String, Object> summaryInstances(List<String> regions) {
 286:         List<String> listRegion = regions != null ? regions : getRegions();
 287:         List<Callable<RegionInstances>> tasks = new ArrayList<>();
 288:         for (String region : listRegion) {
 289:             tasks.add(() -> instancesWithStatus(region));
 290:         }
 291: 
 292:         Map<String, Integer> ec2ByRegion = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 293:         Map<String, Integer> ec2ByType = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 294:         Map<String, Integer> ec2ByState = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 295:         Map<String, Map<String, Integer>> ec2ByRegionAndStatus = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 296: 
 297:         // AWS ?몄텧? 蹂묐젹, 吏묎퀎???쒖꽌 蹂댁〈 ?⑥씪 ?ㅻ젅??硫깅벑)濡??섑뻾.
 298:         for (RegionInstances ri : runParallel(tasks)) {
 299:             if (ri == null) {
 300:                 continue;
 301:             }
 302:             ec2ByRegion.merge(ri.region(), ri.instances().size(), Integer::sum);
 303:             ec2ByRegionAndStatus.put(ri.region(), new LinkedHashMap<>());
 304:             for (Instance inst : ri.instances()) {
 305:                 ec2ByType.merge(inst.instanceTypeAsString(), 1, Integer::sum);
 306:                 ec2ByState.merge(inst.state() != null ? inst.state().nameAsString() : null, 1, Integer::sum);
 307:                 String status = ri.statusMap().get(inst.instanceId());
 308:                 ec2ByRegionAndStatus.get(ri.region()).merge(status, 1, Integer::sum);
 309:             }
 310:         }
 311: 
 312:         Map<String, Object> items = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 313:         items.put("ec2ByRegion", ec2ByRegion);
 314:         items.put("ec2ByType", ec2ByType);
 315:         items.put("ec2ByState", ec2ByState);
 316:         items.put("ec2ByRegionAndStatus", ec2ByRegionAndStatus);
 317:         return Map.of("items", items);
 318:     }
 319: 
 320:     /** 臾닿낵湲?媛쒖슂: EC2 ?몃깽?좊━? ?곹깭 ?먭?留??ъ슜?쒕떎. */
 321:     @Override
 322:     public Map<String, Object> overview(List<String> regions, TimeRange timeRange) {
 323:         List<String> targets = regions != null ? regions : getRegions();
 324:         List<Callable<OverviewRegion>> tasks = targets.stream()
 325:                 .map(region -> (Callable<OverviewRegion>) () -> overviewForRegion(region)).toList();
 326:         List<OverviewRegion> parts = runParallel(tasks);
 327:         List<Map<String, Object>> collection = new ArrayList<>();
 328:         List<Map<String, Object>> risks = new ArrayList<>();
 329:         int healthy=0, warning=0, critical=0, unobserved=0, stopped=0, total=0;
 330:         for (OverviewRegion p : parts) {
 331:             if (p == null) continue;
 332:             collection.add(p.collection()); risks.addAll(p.risks());
 333:             total += p.total; healthy += p.healthy; warning += p.warning; critical += p.critical; unobserved += p.unobserved; stopped += p.stopped;
 334:         }
 335:         Map<String,Integer> coverage = new LinkedHashMap<>();

 361:     private OverviewRegion overviewForRegion(String region) {
 362:         Instant collected = Instant.now();
 363:         List<Instance> instances;
 364:         Map<String,String> statuses;
 365:         try {
 366:             instances = aws.describeInstances(region);
 367:             statuses = instanceStatuses(region, instances.stream().map(Instance::instanceId).toList());
 368:         } catch (Exception e) {
 369:             return OverviewRegion.unavailable(region, "EC2_UNAVAILABLE");
 370:         }
 371:         List<Map<String,Object>> risks=new ArrayList<>();
 372:         Map<String,Integer> coverage=new LinkedHashMap<>();
 373:         int healthy=0,warning=0,critical=0,unobserved=0,stopped=0;
 374:         for (Instance i : instances) {
 375:             coverage.merge(monitoredBy(i, region), 1, Integer::sum);
 376:             String state=i.state()==null ? "unknown" : i.state().nameAsString(); String check=statuses.get(i.instanceId());
 377:             // ?먯젙 洹쒖튃? ?ㅻ깄???섏쭛(CloudSnapshotService)怨?怨듭쑀?쒕떎 ??蹂듭젣 湲덉?.
 378:             String severity = Ec2StatusRules.classify(state, check);
 379:             switch (severity) {
 380:                 case Ec2StatusRules.SEVERITY_STOPPED -> stopped++;
 381:                 case Ec2StatusRules.SEVERITY_CRITICAL -> critical++;
 382:                 case Ec2StatusRules.SEVERITY_WARNING -> warning++;
 383:                 case Ec2StatusRules.SEVERITY_UNOBSERVED -> unobserved++;
 384:                 default -> healthy++;
 385:             }
 386:             if (!"healthy".equals(severity)) { Map<String,Object> x=overviewInstance(i,region,check,severity); x.put("rank", "critical".equals(severity)?4:"warning".equals(severity)?3:"unobserved".equals(severity)?2:1); risks.add(x); }
 387:         }
 388:         Map<String,Object> ec2 = collectionState("ok", collected.toString(), null);
 389:         Map<String,Object> disabled = collectionState("disabled", null, "ZERO_COST_MODE");
 390:         return new OverviewRegion(Map.of("region",region,"ec2",ec2,"cloudWatch",disabled,"alarms",disabled), instances.size(),healthy,warning,critical,unobserved,stopped,risks,coverage);
 391:     }
 392:     private Map<String,Object> collectionState(String state, String lastCollectedAt, String reason) {
 393:         Map<String,Object> result = new LinkedHashMap<>(); result.put("state", state); result.put("lastCollectedAt", lastCollectedAt); result.put("reason", reason); return result;
 394:     }
 395:     private Map<String,Object> overviewInstance(Instance i,String region,String check,String severity) {
 396:         Map<String,Object> x=new LinkedHashMap<>(); x.put("instanceId",i.instanceId()); x.put("instanceName",i.tags().stream().filter(t->"Name".equals(t.key())).map(t->t.value()).findFirst().orElse("-"));
 397:         x.put("region",region); x.put("availabilityZone",i.placement()==null?null:i.placement().availabilityZone()); x.put("state",i.state()==null?null:i.state().nameAsString()); x.put("statusCheck",check); x.put("cpuAverage",null); x.put("memoryAverage",null); x.put("alarmState",null); x.put("severity",severity);
 398:         List<String> reasons=new ArrayList<>(); if(check!=null&&!"2/2 checks passed".equals(check))reasons.add("EC2_STATUS_CHECK_FAILED"); if(reasons.isEmpty())reasons.add("EC2_STATE_"+x.get("state")); x.put("reasons",reasons); return x;
 399:     }
 400:     private record OverviewRegion(Map<String,Object> collection,int total,int healthy,int warning,int critical,int unobserved,int stopped,List<Map<String,Object>> risks,Map<String,Integer> coverage) {
 401:         static OverviewRegion unavailable(String region,String reason) { Map<String,Object> unavailable=collectionUnavailable(reason); Map<String,Object> disabled=collectionDisabled(); return new OverviewRegion(Map.of("region",region,"ec2",unavailable,"cloudWatch",disabled,"alarms",disabled),0,0,0,0,0,0,List.of(),Map.of()); }
 402:         private static Map<String,Object> collectionUnavailable(String reason) { Map<String,Object> result=new LinkedHashMap<>(); result.put("state","unavailable"); result.put("lastCollectedAt",null); result.put("reason",reason); return result; }

 432:     public Map<String, Object> getEc2OperationalDetail(String instanceId, String region) {
 433:         try {
 434:             Instance instance = aws.findInstance(region, instanceId);
 435:             if (instance == null) throw new ApiError(ErrorCode.INSTANCE_NOT_FOUND);

 440:             result.put("statusChecks", statusChecks(status));

 443:             result.put("volumes", aws.describeVolumesByInstance(region, instanceId).stream().map(this::volumeDetail).toList());
 444:             result.put("collection", Map.of("state", "ok", "region", region, "cloudWatchUsed", false));
 445:             return result;
