 455:     @Override
 456:     public Map<String, Object> getVpcDetail(String vpcId, String region) {
 457:         try {
 458:             Vpc vpc = aws.findVpc(region, vpcId);
 459:             if (vpc == null) throw new ApiError(ErrorCode.VPC_NOT_FOUND);
 460:             List<Subnet> subnets = aws.describeSubnetsByVpc(region, vpcId);
 461:             List<RouteTable> routeTables = aws.describeRouteTablesByVpc(region, vpcId);
 462:             List<SecurityGroup> groups = aws.describeSecurityGroupsByVpc(region, vpcId);
 463:             List<Instance> instances = aws.describeInstancesByVpc(region, vpcId);
 464: 
 465:             List<Map<String, Object>> findings = NetworkExposureScanner.findings(subnets, routeTables, groups);
 466:             Map<String, Object> result = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 467:             result.put("vpc", vpcDetail(vpc, region));
 468:             result.put("subnets", subnets.stream().map(this::subnetDetail).toList());
 469:             result.put("routeTables", routeTables.stream().map(this::routeTableDetail).toList());
 470:             result.put("internetGateways", aws.describeInternetGatewaysByVpc(region, vpcId).stream()
 471:                     .map(g -> Map.of("internetGatewayId", g.internetGatewayId(), "state", g.attachments().stream()
 472:                             .map(a -> a.stateAsString()).findFirst().orElse("unknown"))).toList());
 473:             result.put("natGateways", aws.describeNatGatewaysByVpc(region, vpcId).stream().map(n -> {
 474:                 Map<String, Object> x = new LinkedHashMap<>(); x.put("natGatewayId", n.natGatewayId()); x.put("state", n.stateAsString());
 475:                 x.put("subnetId", n.subnetId()); x.put("connectivityType", n.connectivityTypeAsString()); return x;
 476:             }).toList());
 477:             result.put("securityGroups", groups.stream().map(this::securityGroupDetail).toList());
 478:             result.put("instances", instances.stream().map(this::instanceDetail).toList());
 479:             result.put("exposureFindings", findings);
 480:             result.put("collection", Map.of("state", "ok", "region", region, "cloudWatchUsed", false));
 481:             return result;
 482:         } catch (Ec2Exception e) {
 483:             if (awsNotFoundError("vpc", e.awsErrorDetails() == null ? null : e.awsErrorDetails().errorCode())
 484:                     == ErrorCode.VPC_NOT_FOUND) {
 485:                 throw new ApiError(ErrorCode.VPC_NOT_FOUND);
 486:             }
 487:             throw e;
 488:         }
 489:     }

 491:     /**
 492:      * AWS SDK??resource-specific not-found留??덉젙??DMOVE API ?ㅻ쪟濡?諛붽씔??
 493:      *
 494:      * <p>{@code .Malformed}??not-found濡?痍④툒?쒕떎 ??AWS???뺤떇???닿툔??ID??NotFound ???
 495:      * Malformed瑜??섏??붾뜲(?고????ㅼ륫 2026-08-24: {@code Invalid id} ??500 ?좎텧), ?ъ슜??
 496:      * ?낆옣?먯꽑 ????"洹몃윴 由ъ냼???놁쓬"?대떎. 援щ텇??遊ㅼ옄 ?덈궡媛 ?щ씪吏吏 ?딅뒗??
 497:      */
 498:     static ErrorCode awsNotFoundError(String resource, String awsErrorCode) {
 499:         if (awsErrorCode == null || !(awsErrorCode.endsWith(".NotFound") || awsErrorCode.endsWith(".Malformed"))) {
 500:             return null;
 501:         }
 502:         return "instance".equals(resource) && awsErrorCode.contains("Instance")
 503:                 ? ErrorCode.INSTANCE_NOT_FOUND
 504:                 : "vpc".equals(resource) && awsErrorCode.contains("Vpc")
 505:                         ? ErrorCode.VPC_NOT_FOUND : null;
 506:     }

 508:     @Override
 509:     public Map<String, Object> getCloudTrailEvents(String region, String resourceId, String eventName, int maxResults) {
 510:         try {
 511:             Instant end = Instant.now();
 512:             List<Map<String, Object>> items = aws.lookupEvents(region, resourceId, eventName, maxResults,
 513:                     end.minus(java.time.Duration.ofDays(90)), end).stream().map(event -> {
 514:                 Map<String, Object> x = new LinkedHashMap<>(); x.put("eventId", event.eventId()); x.put("eventName", event.eventName());
 515:                 x.put("eventTime", event.eventTime() == null ? null : event.eventTime().toString()); x.put("username", event.username());
 516:                 x.put("resources", event.resources().stream().map(resource -> Map.of("type", resource.resourceType(), "name", resource.resourceName())).toList());
 517:                 x.put("readOnly", event.readOnly()); x.put("source", event.eventSource()); return x;
 518:             }).toList();
 519:             return Map.of("items", items, "collection", Map.of("state", "ok", "region", region,
 520:                     "retentionDays", 90, "nextTokenAvailable", false));
 521:         } catch (Exception e) {
 522:             log.warn("CloudTrail event history unavailable in {}: {}", region, e.getMessage());
 523:             return Map.of("items", List.of(), "collection", Map.of("state", "unavailable", "region", region,
 524:                     "reason", "CLOUDTRAIL_EVENT_HISTORY_UNAVAILABLE", "retentionDays", 90));
 525:         }
 526:     }

 528:     private Map<String, Object> instanceDetail(Instance i) {
 529:         Map<String, Object> x = new LinkedHashMap<>(); x.put("instanceId", i.instanceId());
 530:         x.put("name", i.tags().stream().filter(t -> "Name".equals(t.key())).map(t -> t.value()).findFirst().orElse("-"));
 531:         x.put("state", i.state() == null ? null : i.state().nameAsString()); x.put("instanceType", i.instanceTypeAsString());
 532:         x.put("availabilityZone", i.placement() == null ? null : i.placement().availabilityZone()); x.put("vpcId", i.vpcId());
 533:         x.put("subnetId", i.subnetId()); x.put("privateIp", i.privateIpAddress()); x.put("publicIp", i.publicIpAddress());
 534:         return x;
 535:     }

 537:     private Map<String, Object> statusChecks(InstanceStatus status) {
 538:         Map<String, Object> x = new LinkedHashMap<>(); x.put("overall", status == null ? "unobserved" : instanceStatusesFrom(status));
 539:         x.put("system", status == null || status.systemStatus() == null ? "unobserved" : status.systemStatus().statusAsString());
 540:         x.put("instance", status == null || status.instanceStatus() == null ? "unobserved" : status.instanceStatus().statusAsString()); return x;
 541:     }
 542: 
 543:     private String instanceStatusesFrom(InstanceStatus s) {
 544:         return Ec2StatusRules.overallStatus(s);
 545:     }
 546: 
 547:     private List<Map<String, Object>> scheduledEvents(InstanceStatus status) {
 548:         if (status == null) return List.of();
 549:         return status.events().stream().map(e -> { Map<String, Object> x = new LinkedHashMap<>(); x.put("code", e.codeAsString());
 550:             x.put("description", e.description()); x.put("notBefore", e.notBefore() == null ? null : e.notBefore().toString());
 551:             x.put("notAfter", e.notAfter() == null ? null : e.notAfter().toString()); return x; }).toList();
 552:     }

 558:     private Map<String, Object> routeTableDetail(RouteTable r) { Map<String,Object> x=new LinkedHashMap<>();x.put("routeTableId",r.routeTableId());x.put("associations",r.associations().stream().map(a->Map.of("subnetId",a.subnetId()==null?"main":a.subnetId(),"main",a.main())).toList());x.put("routes",r.routes().stream().map(this::routeDetail).toList());return x; }
 559:     private Map<String, Object> routeDetail(Route r) { Map<String,Object> x=new LinkedHashMap<>();x.put("destination",r.destinationCidrBlock()!=null?r.destinationCidrBlock():r.destinationIpv6CidrBlock());x.put("gatewayId",r.gatewayId());x.put("natGatewayId",r.natGatewayId());x.put("state",r.stateAsString());return x; }
 560:     private Map<String, Object> securityGroupDetail(SecurityGroup g) { Map<String,Object> x=new LinkedHashMap<>();x.put("groupId",g.groupId());x.put("name",g.groupName());x.put("description",g.description());x.put("inbound",g.ipPermissions().stream().map(this::permissionDetail).toList());return x; }
 561:     private Map<String, Object> permissionDetail(IpPermission p) { Map<String,Object> x=new LinkedHashMap<>();x.put("protocol",p.ipProtocol());x.put("fromPort",p.fromPort());x.put("toPort",p.toPort());x.put("ipv4Cidrs",p.ipRanges().stream().map(r->r.cidrIp()).toList());x.put("ipv6Cidrs",p.ipv6Ranges().stream().map(r->r.cidrIpv6()).toList());return x; }
 562: 
 563:     private Map<String, String> instanceStatuses(String region, List<String> instanceIds) {
 564:         Map<String, String> statusMap = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 565:         if (instanceIds.isEmpty()) {
 566:             return statusMap;
 567:         }
 568:         List<InstanceStatus> statuses = aws.describeInstanceStatuses(region, instanceIds);
 569:         for (InstanceStatus s : statuses) {
 570:             int passed = 0;
 571:             if (s.systemStatus() != null && "ok".equals(s.systemStatus().statusAsString())) {
 572:                 passed++;
 573:             }
 574:             if (s.instanceStatus() != null && "ok".equals(s.instanceStatus().statusAsString())) {
 575:                 passed++;
 576:             }
 577:             String check = passed == 2 ? "2/2 checks passed" : passed == 1 ? "1/2 checks failed" : "0/2 checks failed";
 578:             statusMap.put(s.instanceId(), check);
 579:         }
 580:         return statusMap;
 581:     }
 582: 
 583:     private Map<String, String> vpcNameMap(String region, List<String> vpcIds) {
 584:         Map<String, String> map = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 585:         if (vpcIds.isEmpty()) {
 586:             return map;
 587:         }
 588:         for (Vpc vpc : aws.describeVpcs(region, vpcIds)) {
 589:             String name = vpc.tags().stream().filter(t -> "Name".equals(t.key()))
 590:                     .map(t -> t.value()).findFirst().orElse(vpc.vpcId());
 591:             map.put(vpc.vpcId(), name);
 592:         }
 593:         return map;
 594:     }
 595: 
 596:     private String str(Object o) {
 597:         return o != null ? o.toString() : null;
 598:     }
 599: }

 271:     public List<ResultByTime> getCostAndUsageDailyByService(java.time.LocalDate start, java.time.LocalDate end) {
 272:         CloudCredentialsResolver.CloudCreds db = credentials.dbCredsOrNull();
 273:         try (CostExplorerClient client = CostExplorerClient.builder()
 274:                 .region(Region.US_EAST_1)
 275:                 .credentialsProvider(credentials.credentialsProvider(db)).build()) {
 276:             return client.getCostAndUsage(GetCostAndUsageRequest.builder()
 277:                     .timePeriod(DateInterval.builder().start(start.toString()).end(end.toString()).build())
 278:                     .granularity(Granularity.DAILY)
 279:                     .metrics("UnblendedCost")
 280:                     .groupBy(GroupDefinition.builder().type(GroupDefinitionType.DIMENSION).key("SERVICE").build())
 281:                     .build()).resultsByTime();
 282:         }
 283:     }

 285:     private Filter vpcFilter(String vpcId) {
 286:         return Filter.builder().name("vpc-id").values(vpcId).build();
 287:     }
 288: 
 289:     private List<Instance> instances(software.amazon.awssdk.services.ec2.model.DescribeInstancesResponse response) {
 290:         return response.reservations().stream().flatMap(reservation -> reservation.instances().stream()).toList();
 291:     }
 292: 
 293:     private <T> T withEc2(String region, java.util.function.Function<Ec2Client, T> callback) {
 294:         try (Ec2Client client = ec2Client(region)) {
 295:             return callback.apply(client);
 296:         }
 297:     }
 298: 
 299:     private Ec2Client ec2Client(String region) {
 300:         CloudCredentialsResolver.CloudCreds db = credentials.dbCredsOrNull();
 301:         return Ec2Client.builder().region(Region.of(credentials.resolveRegion(region)))
 302:                 .credentialsProvider(credentials.credentialsProvider(db)).build();
 303:     }
 304: 
 305:     private CloudTrailClient cloudTrailClient(String region) {
 306:         CloudCredentialsResolver.CloudCreds db = credentials.dbCredsOrNull();
 307:         return CloudTrailClient.builder().region(Region.of(credentials.resolveRegion(region)))
 308:                 .credentialsProvider(credentials.credentialsProvider(db)).build();
 309:     }
 310: 
 311:     private CloudWatchClient cloudWatchClient(String region) {
 312:         CloudCredentialsResolver.CloudCreds db = credentials.dbCredsOrNull();
 313:         return CloudWatchClient.builder().region(Region.of(credentials.resolveRegion(region)))
 314:                 .credentialsProvider(credentials.credentialsProvider(db)).build();
 315:     }
 316: }

  32:   critical: "rgba(255, 77, 79, 0.20)",
  33:   warning: "rgba(250, 173, 20, 0.20)",
  34:   unobserved: "rgba(140, 140, 140, 0.16)",
  35:   stopped: "rgba(89, 126, 247, 0.14)",
  36: };
  37: 
  38: type StateBand = { start: number; end: number; severity: string };
  39: 
  40: /** ?곗냽 ?숈씪 ?깃툒 ?ㅻ깄?룹쓣 諛대뱶 援ш컙?쇰줈 ?묐뒗?? healthy??諛곌꼍 ?놁쓬(諛대뱶???댁긽 援ш컙留?. */
  41: const toStateBands = (items: TEc2StateHistory["items"]): StateBand[] => {
  42:   const bands: StateBand[] = [];
  43:   items.forEach((item, index) => {
  44:     const start = new Date(item.runAt).getTime();
  45:     const next = items[index + 1];
  46:     const end = next
  47:       ? new Date(next.runAt).getTime()
  48:       : start + SNAPSHOT_INTERVAL_MS;
  49:     const last = bands[bands.length - 1];
  50:     if (last && last.severity === item.severity && last.end >= start) {
  51:       last.end = end;
  52:     } else {
  53:       bands.push({ start, end, severity: item.severity });
  54:     }
  55:   });
  56:   return bands.filter((band) => band.severity !== "healthy");
  57: };
  58: 
  59: type InstanceTimelineProps = {
  60:   instanceId: string;
  61:   region: string;
  62: };
  63: 
  64: const InstanceTimeline = ({ instanceId, region }: InstanceTimelineProps) => {
  65:   const { t } = useTranslation();
  66:   const echartsTheme = useEchartsTheme();
  67:   const [range, setRange] = useState<TimelineRange>("24h");
  68: 
  69:   // 議고쉶 李쎌? range ?좏깮 ?쒖젏 湲곗??쇰줈 怨좎젙 ???뚮뜑留덈떎 epoch媛 ?먮Ⅴ硫?荑쇰━?ㅺ? 怨꾩냽 諛붾먮떎.
  70:   const window = useMemo(() => {
  71:     const to = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
  72:     return { from: to - RANGE_HOURS[range] * 3600, to };
  73:   }, [range]);
  74: 
  75:   const historyQuery = useDmoveQuery<TEc2StateHistory>({
  76:     queryKey: [CLOUD_INFRAS_QUERY_KEY.STATE_HISTORY, instanceId, region, range],
  77:     queryFn: () =>
  78:       CloudInfrasService.getEc2StateHistory({ instanceId, region, ...window }),
  79:     enabled: Boolean(instanceId && region),
  80:   });
  81:   const metricsQuery = useDmoveQuery<TEc2Metrics>({
  82:     queryKey: [CLOUD_INFRAS_QUERY_KEY.EC2_METRICS, instanceId, region, range],
  83:     queryFn: () =>
  84:       CloudInfrasService.getEc2Metrics({ instanceId, region, ...window }),
  85:     enabled: Boolean(instanceId && region),
  86:   });
  87:   const eventsQuery = useDmoveQuery<TCloudTrailEventHistory>({
  88:     queryKey: [CLOUD_INFRAS_QUERY_KEY.CLOUDTRAIL_EVENTS, "timeline", instanceId, region],
  89:     queryFn: () =>
  90:       CloudInfrasService.getCloudTrailEvents({ region, resourceId: instanceId }),
  91:     enabled: Boolean(instanceId && region),
  92:   });
  93: 
  94:   const bands = useMemo(
  95:     () => toStateBands(historyQuery.data?.items ?? []),
  96:     [historyQuery.data],
  97:   );
  98: 
  99:   /** 議고쉶 李??덉쓽 ?곌린(write) ?대깽?몃쭔 留덉빱濡????쎄린 ?대깽?멸퉴吏 李띿쑝硫???諛섏씠 留덉빱媛 ?쒕떎. */
 100:   const eventMarkers = useMemo(() => {
 101:     const fromMs = window.from * 1000;
 102:     return (eventsQuery.data?.items ?? [])
 103:       // readOnly??CloudTrail ?먭컪 洹몃?濡?臾몄옄??"true"/"false")?대떎 ??boolean 鍮꾧탳???곸썝??miss.
 104:       .filter((event) => event.readOnly === "false" && event.eventTime)
 105:       .filter((event) => new Date(event.eventTime as string).getTime() >= fromMs)
 106:       .map((event) => ({
 107:         xAxis: new Date(event.eventTime as string).getTime(),
 108:         label: { formatter: event.eventName, position: "insideEndTop" as const },
 109:       }));
 110:   }, [eventsQuery.data, window.from]);
 111: 
 112:   const markArea: MarkAreaComponentOption = {
 113:     silent: true,
 114:     data: bands.map((band) => [
 115:       { xAxis: band.start, itemStyle: { color: SEVERITY_BAND_COLOR[band.severity] } },
 116:       { xAxis: band.end },
 117:     ]),
 118:   };
 119:   const markLine = {
 120:     silent: true,
 121:     symbol: "none",
 122:     lineStyle: { type: "dashed" as const, width: 1 },
 123:     data: eventMarkers,
 124:   };
 125: 
 126:   // 濡쒖슦?곗씠??酉곗뼱("李⑦듃??濡쒖슦?곗씠??蹂????덇쾶") ??諛대뱶 ?꾩슜 ?ㅽ듃由쎌뿏 ?ｌ? ?딅뒗???섏튂 ?쒕━利??놁쓬).
 127:   const rawDataToolbox = dataViewToolbox(dataViewLabels(t));
 128: 
 129:   const baseOption = (overrides: EChartsOption): EChartsOption => ({
 130:     tooltip: { trigger: "axis" },
 131:     toolbox: rawDataToolbox,
 132:     grid: { left: 56, right: 24, top: 32, bottom: 28 },
 133:     xAxis: {
 134:       type: "time",
 135:       min: window.from * 1000,
 136:       max: window.to * 1000,
 137:     },
 138:     ...overrides,
 139:   });
 140: 
 141:   const toSeriesData = (points: { time: string; value: number }[]) =>
 142:     points.map((point) => [new Date(point.time).getTime(), point.value]);
 143: 
 144:   const metrics = metricsQuery.data;
 145:   const metricsOk = metrics?.collection.state === "ok";
 146:   const degradedReason = metrics && !metricsOk ? metrics.collection.reason : null;
 147:   // ?뚯뒪 留ㅽ듃由?뒪(D11): agent硫?CPU+硫붾え由??ъ슜瑜?Zabbix), CW硫?5吏?? 諛곗?濡???긽 ?대뵒????媛믪씤吏 諛앺엺??
 148:   const source = metrics?.collection.source;
 149:   const isAgentSource = source === "zabbix-agent";
 150:   const fallbackUsed = metrics?.collection.fallbackUsed === true;
 151: 
 152:   const cpuOption = baseOption({
 153:     yAxis: { type: "value", axisLabel: { formatter: "{value}%" }, max: 100 },
 154:     series: [
 155:       {
 156:         name: "CPU",
 157:         type: "line",
 158:         symbol: "none",
 159:         sampling: "lttb",
 160:         data: toSeriesData(metrics?.items.cpu ?? []),
 161:         markArea,
 162:         markLine,
 163:       },
 164:     ],
 165:   });
 166:   const bytesAxis = {
 167:     yAxis: {
 168:       type: "value" as const,
 169:       axisLabel: { formatter: (value: number) => formatBytesFromRaw(value) },
 170:     },
 171:     tooltip: {
 172:       trigger: "axis" as const,
 173:       valueFormatter: (value: unknown) => formatBytesFromRaw(Number(value)),
 174:     },
 175:   };
 176:   const memoryOption = baseOption({
 177:     yAxis: { type: "value", axisLabel: { formatter: "{value}%" }, max: 100 },
 178:     series: [
 179:       {
 180:         name: t("cloud_timeline.memory_usage"),
 181:         type: "line",
 182:         symbol: "none",
 183:         sampling: "lttb",
 184:         data: toSeriesData(metrics?.items.memoryUsage ?? []),
 185:         markArea,
 186:       },
 187:     ],
 188:   });
 189:   const networkOption = baseOption({
 190:     ...bytesAxis,
 191:     series: [
 192:       { name: t("cloud_timeline.network_in"), type: "line", symbol: "none", sampling: "lttb", data: toSeriesData(metrics?.items.networkIn ?? []), markArea },
 193:       { name: t("cloud_timeline.network_out"), type: "line", symbol: "none", sampling: "lttb", data: toSeriesData(metrics?.items.networkOut ?? []) },
 194:     ],
 195:     legend: { top: 0 },
 196:   });
 197:   const diskOption = baseOption({
 198:     ...bytesAxis,
 199:     series: [
 200:       { name: t("cloud_timeline.disk_read"), type: "line", symbol: "none", sampling: "lttb", data: toSeriesData(metrics?.items.diskReadBytes ?? []), markArea },
 201:       { name: t("cloud_timeline.disk_write"), type: "line", symbol: "none", sampling: "lttb", data: toSeriesData(metrics?.items.diskWriteBytes ?? []) },
 202:     ],
 203:     legend: { top: 0 },
 204:   });
 205:   /** 媛뺣벑 ?쒖뿉??洹몃━???곹깭 諛대뱶 ?꾩슜 ?ㅽ듃由????곗씠???녿뒗 line ?쒕━利덉뿉 markArea/markLine留??ｋ뒗?? */
 206:   const bandOnlyOption = baseOption({
 207:     grid: { left: 56, right: 24, top: 24, bottom: 28, height: 60 },
 208:     yAxis: { type: "value", show: false, min: 0, max: 1 },
 209:     tooltip: { show: false },
 210:     toolbox: { show: false },
 211:     series: [{ type: "line", data: [], markArea, markLine }],
 212:   });
 213: 
 214:   const hasHistory = (historyQuery.data?.items.length ?? 0) > 0;
 215:   const severityLegend = ["critical", "warning", "unobserved", "stopped"] as const;
 216: 
 217:   return (
 218:     <Card
 219:       title={t("cloud_timeline.title")}
 220:       extra={
 221:         <Segmented
 222:           value={range}
 223:           options={Object.keys(RANGE_HOURS)}
 224:           onChange={(value) => setRange(value as TimelineRange)}
 225:         />
 226:       }
 227:     >
 228:       <Flex vertical gap={12}>
 229:         {degradedReason && (
 230:           <Alert
 231:             type="info"
 232:             showIcon
 233:             message={t(`cloud_timeline.degraded_${degradedReason}`)}
 234:           />
 235:         )}
 236:         {/* agent 留ㅼ묶? ?먮뒗???덉쭏 誘몃떖濡?CW濡??대젮??寃쎌슦 ??議곗슜???섍린吏 ?딄퀬 ?뚮┛??愿痢?怨듬갚 諛⑹?). */}
 237:         {fallbackUsed && (
 238:           <Alert type="warning" showIcon message={t("cloud_timeline.fallback_notice")} />
 239:         )}
 240:         <Flex gap={4} wrap align="center">
 241:           {source && (
 242:             <Tag color={isAgentSource ? "green" : "blue"}>
 243:               {t(`cloud_timeline.source_${isAgentSource ? "agent" : "cloudwatch"}`)}
 244:               {isAgentSource && metrics?.collection.zabbixHostName
 245:                 ? ` 쨌 ${metrics.collection.zabbixHostName}`
 246:                 : ""}
 247:             </Tag>
 248:           )}
 249:           <Typography.Text type="secondary">
 250:             {t("cloud_timeline.band_legend")}
 251:           </Typography.Text>
 252:           {severityLegend.map((severity) => (
 253:             <Tag key={severity} color={SEVERITY_BAND_COLOR[severity]}>
 254:               {t(`cloud_overview.severity_${severity}`)}
 255:             </Tag>
 256:           ))}
 257:         </Flex>
 258:         {!hasHistory && !historyQuery.isLoading && (
 259:           <Empty
 260:             image={Empty.PRESENTED_IMAGE_SIMPLE}
 261:             description={t("cloud_timeline.no_snapshot")}
 262:           />
 263:         )}
 264:         {metricsOk ? (
 265:           <>
 266:             <Typography.Text strong>{t("cloud_timeline.cpu")}</Typography.Text>
 267:             <ReactECharts option={cpuOption} theme={echartsTheme} style={{ height: 220 }} notMerge />
 268:             {isAgentSource ? (
 269:               // agent ?뚯뒪: CW 湲곕낯吏?쒖뿏 ?녿뒗 硫붾え由??ъ슜瑜좎씠 ?섏삩?????먯씠?꾪듃 ?ㅼ튂??泥닿컧 媛移?
 270:               <>
 271:                 <Typography.Text strong>{t("cloud_timeline.memory_usage")}</Typography.Text>
 272:                 <ReactECharts option={memoryOption} theme={echartsTheme} style={{ height: 220 }} notMerge />
 273:               </>
 274:             ) : (
 275:               <>
 276:                 <Typography.Text strong>{t("cloud_timeline.network")}</Typography.Text>
 277:                 <ReactECharts option={networkOption} theme={echartsTheme} style={{ height: 220 }} notMerge />
 278:                 <Typography.Text strong>{t("cloud_timeline.disk")}</Typography.Text>
 279:                 <ReactECharts option={diskOption} theme={echartsTheme} style={{ height: 220 }} notMerge />
 280:               </>
 281:             )}
 282:           </>
 283:         ) : (
 284:           hasHistory && (
 285:             <>
 286:               <Typography.Text strong>{t("cloud_timeline.state_band")}</Typography.Text>
 287:               <ReactECharts option={bandOnlyOption} theme={echartsTheme} style={{ height: 140 }} notMerge />
 288:             </>
 289:           )
 290:         )}
 291:       </Flex>
 292:     </Card>
 293:   );
 294: };
 295: 
 296: export default InstanceTimeline;

  24: const MONITORING_SOURCE_COLOR: Record<string, string> = {

  41: const EC2InstanceList = () => {

  44:

  46:   const vpcIdParams = getQueryStringByKey(QueryParamKeys.VPC_IDS);
  47: 
  48:   const queryParams = (() => {
  49:     if (regionsParams && vpcIdParams) return `${regionsParams}&${vpcIdParams}`;
  50:     if (regionsParams) return regionsParams;
  51:     if (vpcIdParams) return vpcIdParams;
  52:     return "";
  53:   })();
  54: 
  55:   const { data: VPCData, isLoading: isVPCLoading } = useDmoveQuery<
  56:     TResponseList<TVpc>
  57:   >({
  58:     queryKey: [CLOUD_INFRAS_QUERY_KEY.VPCS_LIST],
  59:     queryFn: () => CloudInfrasService.getListVpcs({}),
  60:     enabled: !!stringParams,
  61:   });
  62:

  64:     queryKey: [CLOUD_INFRAS_QUERY_KEY.EC2S_LIST, queryParams],
  65:     queryFn: () => CloudInfrasService.getListEc2s({ params: queryParams }),
  66:     enabled: !!stringParams,
  67:     refetchInterval: TEN_MINUTES,
  68:   });
  69: 
  70:   const regionFilter = useFilterRegion();
  71:

 127:     {
 128:       key: "instance_type",
 129:       dataIndex: "instanceType",
 130:       render: (value) => renderDisplayValue({ value: value }),

 138:           if (value.includes("failed")) return "red";
 139:           return "default";
 140:         })();
 141:         return renderDisplayValue({
 142:           value: value,

 199:     { key: "check", label: t("cloud_list_kpi.failed_status_check"), value: failedCheckCount, tone: failedCheckCount ? "critical" : "default", loading: isLoading },

 233:           selectedInstance && (

 244:             instanceId={selectedInstance.instanceId}
 245:             region={selectedInstance.region}
 246:           />
 247:         )}
 248:       </ResizableDrawer>
 249:     </>
 250:   );
 251: };
 252: 
 253: export default EC2InstanceList;

### src/pages/cloud-infra-monitoring/aws-resources/EC2Dashboard.tsx
   1: import { dataViewLabels, dataViewToolbox } from "../components/chartDataView";
   2: import CloudBillingCard from "../components/CloudBillingCard";
   3: import { StatCardRow, TStatCardProps } from "@/components/StatCard/StatCard";
   4: import DmoveTableFilter from "@/components/Table/DmoveTableFilter";
   5: import { TEN_MINUTES } from "@/constants";
   6: import { CLOUD_INFRAS_QUERY_KEY } from "@/constants/queryKey";
   7: import { QueryParamKeys } from "@/enums/common";
   8: import { useFilterRegion } from "@/hooks/options/useFilterRegion";
   9: import useDmoveQuery from "@/hooks/useDmoveQuery";
  10: import { useQueryParams } from "@/hooks/useQueryParams";
  11: import CloudInfrasService from "@/services/cloud-infra";
  12: import { TCloudOverview } from "@/types/cloud-infra";
  13: import { TResponse } from "@/types/dataResponse";
  14: import { formatBytesFromRaw } from "@/utils";
  15: import { Alert, Card, Col, Empty, Flex, Progress, Row, Segmented, Space, Table, Tag, Typography } from "antd";
  16: import { ColumnsType } from "antd/es/table";
  17: import type { EChartsOption } from "echarts";
  18: import ReactECharts from "echarts-for-react";
  19: import useEchartsTheme from "@/hooks/useEchartsTheme";
  20: import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";
  21: 
  22: const severityColor = { critical: "error", warning: "warning", unobserved: "default", stopped: "default" } as const;
  23: const COVERAGE_COLOR = { "zabbix-agent": "green", cloudwatch: "blue", unavailable: "orange", unmonitored: "default" } as const;
  24: const collectionColor = { ok: "success", partial: "warning", unavailable: "error", disabled: "default" } as const;
  25: 
  26: const EC2Dashboard = () => {
  27:   const { t } = useTranslation();
  28:   const { getQueryStringByKey, searchParams, updateParams } = useQueryParams();
  29:   const regionParams = getQueryStringByKey(QueryParamKeys.REGIONS);
  30:   const range = searchParams.get("range") ?? "1h";
  31:   const params = [regionParams, `range=${range}`].filter(Boolean).join("&");
  32:   const filter = useFilterRegion();
  33:   const { data, isLoading, isError } = useDmoveQuery<TResponse<TCloudOverview>>({
  34:     queryKey: [CLOUD_INFRAS_QUERY_KEY.OVERVIEW, params],
  35:     queryFn: () => CloudInfrasService.getOverview({ params }),
  36:     refetchInterval: TEN_MINUTES,
  37:   });
  38:   const summary = data?.summary;
  39:   const cards: (TStatCardProps & { key: string })[] = [
  40:     { key: "total", label: t("cloud_overview.total_instances"), value: summary?.totalInstances ?? 0, loading: isLoading },
  41:     { key: "critical", label: t("cloud_overview.critical"), value: summary?.criticalInstances ?? 0, tone: summary?.criticalInstances ? "critical" : "default", loading: isLoading },
  42:     { key: "warning", label: t("cloud_overview.warning"), value: summary?.warningInstances ?? 0, tone: summary?.warningInstances ? "warning" : "default", loading: isLoading },
  43:     { key: "healthy", label: t("cloud_overview.healthy"), value: summary?.healthyInstances ?? 0, tone: summary?.healthyInstances ? "good" : "default", loading: isLoading },
  44:     { key: "alarms", label: t("cloud_overview.active_alarms"), value: summary?.activeAlarms ?? 0, tone: summary?.activeAlarms ? "critical" : "default", loading: isLoading },
  45:   ];
  46:   const riskColumns: ColumnsType<TCloudOverview["riskResources"][number]> = [
  47:     { title: t("cloud_overview.resource"), key: "resource", render: (_, row) => <Space direction="vertical" size={0}><Typography.Text strong>{row.instanceName || row.instanceId}</Typography.Text><Typography.Text type="secondary">{row.instanceId}</Typography.Text></Space> },
  48:     { title: t("region"), dataIndex: "region", key: "region" },
  49:     { title: t("cloud_overview.severity"), dataIndex: "severity", key: "severity", render: (value) => <Tag color={severityColor[value]}>{t(`cloud_overview.severity_${value}`)}</Tag> },
  50:     { title: t("cloud_overview.reason"), dataIndex: "reasons", key: "reasons", render: (reasons: string[]) => reasons.join(", ") || "-" },
  51:     { title: t("cloud_overview.cpu_average"), dataIndex: "cpuAverage", key: "cpuAverage", render: (value: number | null) => value === null ? "-" : `${value.toFixed(1)}%` },
  52:   ];
  53:   // ?뵶 "?ㅽ뙣"??unavailable/partial留뚯씠?? disabled??臾닿낵湲?紐⑤뱶?먯꽌 CloudWatch瑜??쇰?????遺瑜??곹깭??  //    BE媛 紐⑤뱺 由ъ쟾????긽 ?대젮以?????닿구 ?ㅽ뙣濡?移섎㈃ 寃쎄퀬媛 100% ?곸떆 ?몄텧?쒕떎(2026-08-26 ?ㅽ깘 ?섏젙).
  54:   const collectionRegions = data?.collection.regions ?? [];
  55:   const hasCollectionProblem = collectionRegions.some((region) =>
  56:     [region.ec2, region.cloudWatch, region.alarms].some(({ state }) => state === "unavailable" || state === "partial"));
  57:   const cloudWatchDisabled = collectionRegions.length > 0 && collectionRegions.every((region) => region.cloudWatch.state === "disabled");
  58: 
  59:   // ?뚮┸ ?곹깭 異붿씠 ???ㅻ깄??吏묎퀎(trends.instanceStates). AWS 異붽? ?몄텧 ?놁씠 ?쒓컙 異뺤씠 ?앷릿??
  60:   const echartsTheme = useEchartsTheme();
  61:   const stateTrend = data?.trends.instanceStates ?? [];
  62:   const trendOption: EChartsOption = {
  63:     tooltip: { trigger: "axis" },
  64:     toolbox: dataViewToolbox(dataViewLabels(t)),
  65:     legend: { top: 0 },
  66:     grid: { left: 40, right: 16, top: 32, bottom: 28 },
  67:     xAxis: { type: "time" },
  68:     yAxis: { type: "value", minInterval: 1 },
  69:     series: (["critical", "warning", "unobserved", "stopped", "healthy"] as const).map((key) => ({
  70:       name: t(`cloud_overview.severity_${key}`),
  71:       type: "line",
  72:       stack: "fleet",
  73:       areaStyle: {},
  74:       symbol: "none",
  75:       data: stateTrend.map((bucket) => [new Date(bucket.time).getTime(), bucket[key]]),
  76:     })),
  77:   };
  78: 
  79:   return <Flex vertical gap={16}>
  80:     <DmoveTableFilter isShowSearch={false} filter={[filter]} />
  81:     <Flex justify="space-between" align="center" wrap gap={12}><Typography.Title level={3} className="m-0">{t("cloud_overview.title")}</Typography.Title><Segmented value={range} options={["1h", "6h", "24h", "7d"]} onChange={(value) => updateParams([{ key: "range", value: String(value) }])} /></Flex>
  82:     {isError && <Alert type="error" showIcon message={t("cloud_overview.load_failed")} />}
  83:     {hasCollectionProblem && <Alert type="warning" showIcon message={t("cloud_overview.partial_collection")} />}
  84:     {!hasCollectionProblem && cloudWatchDisabled && <Alert type="info" showIcon message={t("cloud_overview.cloudwatch_disabled_notice")} />}
  85:     <StatCardRow items={cards} />
  86:     {/* ?뚯뒪 留ㅽ듃由?뒪 而ㅻ쾭由ъ? ???먯씠?꾪듃 ?ㅼ튂 ?좊룄(?쒗뭹 蹂몃쪟)?댁옄 怨좉컼??CloudWatch 鍮꾩슜 ?덇컧 ?덈궡. */}
  87:     {summary?.monitoringCoverage && (
  88:       <Card size="small" title={t("cloud_coverage.title")}>
  89:         <Flex gap={8} wrap align="center">
  90:           {(["zabbix-agent", "cloudwatch", "unavailable", "unmonitored"] as const).map((key) => (
  91:             <Tag key={key} color={COVERAGE_COLOR[key]}>
  92:               {t(`cloud_coverage.${key}`)}: {summary.monitoringCoverage?.[key] ?? 0}
  93:             </Tag>
  94:           ))}
  95:           <Typography.Text type="secondary">{t("cloud_coverage.hint")}</Typography.Text>
  96:         </Flex>
  97:       </Card>
  98:     )}
  99:     <Row gutter={[16, 16]}>
 100:       <Col xs={24} xl={14}>
 101:         <Card title={t("cloud_overview.state_trend")} className="h-full">{stateTrend.length ? <ReactECharts option={trendOption} theme={echartsTheme} style={{ height: 260 }} notMerge /> : <Empty image={Empty.PRESENTED_IMAGE_SIMPLE} description={t("cloud_overview.no_trend_data")} />}</Card>
 102:       </Col>
 103:       <Col xs={24} xl={10}>
 104:         {/* ?ㅼ껌援ъ븸 ??鍮꾩슜 ?섏씠吏? 怨듭쑀 而댄룷?뚰듃. ?듯듃??爰쇱쭚(湲곕낯)?대㈃ ?좉툑 移대뱶. */}
 105:         <CloudBillingCard />
 106:       </Col>
 107:     </Row>
 108:     <Row gutter={[16, 16]}>
 109:       <Col xs={24} xl={14}><Card title={t("cloud_overview.risk_resources")}><Table rowKey={(row) => `${row.region}-${row.instanceId}`} columns={riskColumns} dataSource={data?.riskResources ?? []} loading={isLoading} pagination={{ pageSize: 5, hideOnSinglePage: true }} locale={{ emptyText: <Empty description={t("cloud_overview.no_risk_resources")} /> }} /></Card></Col>
 110:       <Col xs={24} xl={10}><Card title={t("cloud_overview.cpu_top")}><Space direction="vertical" className="w-full" size={12}>{(data?.trends.cpuTop ?? []).map((item) => <div key={`${item.region}-${item.instanceId}`}><Flex justify="space-between"><Typography.Text>{item.instanceName || item.instanceId}</Typography.Text><Typography.Text>{item.average.toFixed(1)}%</Typography.Text></Flex><Progress percent={Math.min(100, item.average)} showInfo={false} status={item.average >= 80 ? "exception" : "active"} /></div>)}{!isLoading && !data?.trends.cpuTop.length && <Empty image={Empty.PRESENTED_IMAGE_SIMPLE} description={t("cloud_overview.no_metric_data")} />}</Space></Card></Col>
 111:       <Col xs={24} xl={14}><Card title={t("cloud_overview.active_alarms")}><Table rowKey={(row) => `${row.region}-${row.name}`} size="small" loading={isLoading} pagination={{ pageSize: 5, hideOnSinglePage: true }} dataSource={data?.alarms ?? []} columns={[{ title: t("cloud_overview.alarm"), dataIndex: "name", key: "name" }, { title: t("region"), dataIndex: "region", key: "region" }, { title: t("cloud_overview.state"), dataIndex: "state", key: "state", render: (value) => <Tag color={value === "ALARM" ? "error" : "warning"}>{value}</Tag> }]} locale={{ emptyText: <Empty description={t("cloud_overview.no_active_alarms")} /> }} /></Card></Col>
 112:       <Col xs={24} xl={10}><Card title={t("cloud_overview.network_top")}><Space direction="vertical" className="w-full" size={10}>{(data?.trends.networkTop ?? []).map((item) => <Flex key={`${item.region}-${item.instanceId}`} justify="space-between" gap={12}><Typography.Text ellipsis>{item.instanceName || item.instanceId}</Typography.Text><Typography.Text>{formatBytesFromRaw(item.totalBytesPerSecond)}/s</Typography.Text></Flex>)}{!isLoading && !data?.trends.networkTop.length && <Empty image={Empty.PRESENTED_IMAGE_SIMPLE} description={t("cloud_overview.no_metric_data")} />}</Space></Card></Col>
 113:     </Row>
 114:     {!!data?.collection.regions.length && <Card size="small" title={t("cloud_overview.collection_status")}><Space wrap>{data.collection.regions.map((region) => {
 115:       const sources = [["EC2", region.ec2], ["CloudWatch", region.cloudWatch], ["Alarm", region.alarms]] as const;
 116:       return <Space key={region.region} direction="vertical" size={2}><Typography.Text strong>{region.region}</Typography.Text>{sources.map(([name, source]) => <Tag key={name} color={collectionColor[source.state]}>{name}: {source.state}{source.reason ? ` (${source.reason})` : ""}</Tag>)}</Space>;
 117:     })}</Space></Card>}
 118:   </Flex>;
 119: };
 120: 
 121: export default EC2Dashboard;

### engine-api/src/main/java/com/dmove/moveseye/engine/model/metric/SeriesQuery.java
   1: package com.dmove.moveseye.engine.model.metric;
   2: 
   3: import com.dmove.moveseye.engine.model.TimeRange;
   4: import com.dmove.moveseye.engine.model.EntityRef;
   5: import java.util.List;
   6: 
   7: /**
   8:  * ?쒓퀎??議고쉶 荑쇰━. devdesign 짠4.
   9:  *
  10:  * <p>?뷀떚??횞 硫뷀듃由?쓽 援먯감怨깆쓣 {@code range}쨌{@code rollup}?쇰줈 議고쉶?쒕떎(Phase 1 ?뚮퉬?먮뒗 蹂댄넻
  11:  * ?⑥씪 ?뷀떚??+ ?뚯닔 硫뷀듃由?. proto: {@code message SeriesQuery { repeated EntityRef entities = 1;
  12:  * repeated MetricRef metrics = 2; TimeRange range = 3; Rollup rollup = 4; }}.
  13:  */
  14: public record SeriesQuery(List<EntityRef> entities, List<MetricRef> metrics, TimeRange range, Rollup rollup) {
  15: 
  16:     public SeriesQuery {
  17:         entities = entities == null ? List.of() : List.copyOf(entities);
  18:         metrics = metrics == null ? List.of() : List.copyOf(metrics);
  19:         if (rollup == null) {
  20:             rollup = Rollup.RAW;
  21:         }
  22:     }
  23: 
  24:     /** ?⑥씪 ?뷀떚?걔룸떒??硫뷀듃由??몄쓽 ?앹꽦?? */
  25:     public static SeriesQuery of(EntityRef entity, MetricRef metric, TimeRange range, Rollup rollup) {
  26:         return new SeriesQuery(List.of(entity), List.of(metric), range, rollup);
  27:     }
  28: }

### src/main/java/com/dmove/moveseye/aws/dto/CloudOverviewQueryDto.java
   1: package com.dmove.moveseye.aws.dto;
   2: 
   3: import com.dmove.moveseye.common.exception.ApiError;
   4: import com.dmove.moveseye.common.exception.ErrorCode;
   5: import java.time.Duration;
   6: import java.time.Instant;
   7: import java.time.format.DateTimeParseException;
   8: import java.util.Arrays;
   9: import java.util.List;
  10: import lombok.Getter;
  11: import lombok.Setter;
  12: 
  13: /** GET /cloud-infras/overview query. 湲곌컙 ?댁꽍? ?쒕쾭留??섑뻾?쒕떎. */
  14: @Getter
  15: @Setter
  16: public class CloudOverviewQueryDto {
  17:     private String regions;
  18:     private String range;
  19:     private String from;
  20:     private String to;
  21: 
  22:     public List<String> getRegionList() {
  23:         if (regions == null || regions.isBlank()) return null;
  24:         List<String> result = Arrays.stream(regions.split(",")).map(String::trim).filter(s -> !s.isBlank()).toList();
  25:         if (result.isEmpty() || result.size() > 20) throw new ApiError(ErrorCode.INVALID_QUERY);
  26:         return result;
  27:     }
  28: 
  29:     public TimeRange resolveTimeRange(Instant now) {
  30:         boolean hasRange = range != null && !range.isBlank();
  31:         boolean hasFrom = from != null && !from.isBlank();
  32:         boolean hasTo = to != null && !to.isBlank();
  33:         if (hasRange && (hasFrom || hasTo)) throw new ApiError(ErrorCode.INVALID_QUERY);
  34:         if (hasFrom != hasTo) throw new ApiError(ErrorCode.INVALID_QUERY);
  35:         if (hasRange) {
  36:             Duration d = switch (range) {
  37:                 case "1h" -> Duration.ofHours(1); case "6h" -> Duration.ofHours(6);
  38:                 case "24h" -> Duration.ofHours(24); case "7d" -> Duration.ofDays(7);
  39:                 default -> throw new ApiError(ErrorCode.INVALID_QUERY);
  40:             };
  41:             return new TimeRange(now.minus(d), now, range);
  42:         }
  43:         if (!hasFrom) return new TimeRange(now.minus(Duration.ofHours(1)), now, "1h");
  44:         try {
  45:             Instant start = Instant.parse(from); Instant end = Instant.parse(to);
  46:             if (!start.isBefore(end) || Duration.between(start, end).compareTo(Duration.ofDays(7)) > 0)
  47:                 throw new ApiError(ErrorCode.INVALID_QUERY);
  48:             return new TimeRange(start, end, "custom");
  49:         } catch (DateTimeParseException e) { throw new ApiError(ErrorCode.INVALID_QUERY); }
  50:     }
  51:     public record TimeRange(Instant from, Instant to, String label) { }
  52: }

  84:     @Override
  85:     public List<Series> querySeries(SourceRef src, CallerContext ctx, SeriesQuery query) {
  86:         String token = sessionProvider.resolve(ctx);
  87:         Rollup rollup = query.rollup();
  88:         try {
  89:             BatchResolver resolver = buildResolver(token, query.entities(), query.metrics());
  90:             // 1) ??(?뷀떚?걘쀫찓?몃┃) ?댁꽍 ??? 紐⑸줉(?붿껌 ?쒖꽌 蹂댁〈).
  91:             List<Cell> cells = new ArrayList<>(query.entities().size() * query.metrics().size());
  92:             for (EntityRef entity : query.entities()) {
  93:                 for (MetricRef metric : query.metrics()) {
  94:                     cells.add(new Cell(entity, metric, resolver.resolve(entity.nativeId(), metric)));
  95:                 }
  96:             }
  97:             // 2) ?쒓퀎??諛곗튂(?ㅽ뀦 3b): RAW=history瑜?value_type ?뚯씠釉붾퀎 1肄? HOURLY_TREND=trend 1肄쒕줈 諛쏆븘
  98:             //    itemid?믫룷?명듃濡?遺꾨같?쒕떎. 硫뷀듃由?쭏??媛쒕퀎 history.get???좊━??N+1(iface ?ㅼ닔 ?붿빟)???쒓굅.
  99:             //    ?⑥씪 硫뷀듃由??뚮퉬??cpu/memory/disk ?쇱씤李⑦듃)???ъ쟾???뺥솗??1肄??숈옉 遺덈?).
 100:             Map<String, List<SeriesPoint>> pointsByItem = fetchPointsBatched(token, cells, query, rollup);
 101:             // 3) ? ?쒖꽌?濡?Series 議곕┰. nativeId=itemid(?쇱씤李⑦듃 point.itemid 臾댁넀??蹂댁〈, 짠D4/?ㅽ뀦 2).
 102:             List<Series> out = new ArrayList<>(cells.size());
 103:             for (Cell c : cells) {
 104:                 String itemid = c.item == null ? null : c.item.itemid();
 105:                 List<SeriesPoint> points = itemid == null
 106:                         ? List.of()
 107:                         : pointsByItem.getOrDefault(itemid, List.of());
 108:                 out.add(new Series(c.entity, c.metric, itemid, points));
 109:             }
 110:             return out;
 111:         } catch (RuntimeException e) {
 112:             throw translate(e);
 113:         }
 114:     }

 116:     /** ?댁꽍??(?뷀떚?? 硫뷀듃由? ?꾩씠?? ?. item? 誘명빐????null. */
 117:     private record Cell(EntityRef entity, MetricRef metric, ZabbixBasicItem item) {
 118:     }
 119: 
 120:     /**
 121:      * querySeries???쒓퀎??諛곗튂 議고쉶(?ㅽ뀦 3b). itemid ???ъ씤??留듭쓣 諛섑솚?쒕떎.
 122:      * <ul>
 123:      *   <li>RAW: ?댁꽍???꾩씠?쒖쓣 {@code value_type} ?덉뒪?좊━ ?뚯씠釉붾퀎濡?臾띠뼱 ?뚯씠釉붾떦 {@code history.get} 1肄?
 124:      *       (?쇳빀 ????꾩씠?쒖쓣 1肄쒖뿉 ?욎쑝硫?Zabbix媛 遺덉씪移???낆쓣 鍮?寃곌낵濡?二쇰?濡?諛섎뱶??遺꾨━ ???ㅽ뀦 2 ?뚭?).</li>
 125:      *   <li>HOURLY_TREND: ??itemid瑜?{@code trend.get} 1肄쒕줈.</li>
 126:      * </ul>
 127:      * 媛??꾩씠?쒖쓽 ?ъ씤???쒖꽌???뚯뒪 諛섑솚 ?쒖꽌(history.get? {@code sortfield=clock ASC})瑜?蹂댁〈?쒕떎.
 128:      */
 129:     private Map<String, List<SeriesPoint>> fetchPointsBatched(String token, List<Cell> cells,
 130:                                                               SeriesQuery query, Rollup rollup) {
 131:         long from = query.range().fromEpoch();
 132:         long to = query.range().toEpoch();
 133:         Map<String, List<SeriesPoint>> byItem = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 134:         if (rollup == Rollup.HOURLY_TREND) {
 135:             List<String> itemids = distinctItemids(cells);
 136:             if (itemids.isEmpty()) {
 137:                 return byItem;
 138:             }
 139:             for (TrendPoint t : trendService.getTrendByItemids(token, from, to, itemids)) {
 140:                 byItem.computeIfAbsent(t.itemid(), k -> new ArrayList<>())
 141:                         .add(new SeriesPoint(toEpoch(t.timestamp()), t.valueAvg()));
 142:             }
 143:             return byItem;
 144:         }
 145:         // RAW: value_type ?덉뒪?좊━ ?뚯씠釉붾퀎濡?itemid 洹몃９??以묐났 itemid 1??.
 146:         Map<Integer, List<String>> idsByHistoryType = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 147:         Set<String> seen = new LinkedHashSet<>();
 148:         for (Cell c : cells) {
 149:             if (c.item == null || !seen.add(c.item.itemid())) {
 150:                 continue;
 151:             }
 152:             idsByHistoryType.computeIfAbsent(historyTypeOf(c.item), k -> new ArrayList<>()).add(c.item.itemid());
 153:         }
 154:         for (Map.Entry<Integer, List<String>> e : idsByHistoryType.entrySet()) {
 155:             List<HistoryPoint> history = historyService.getHistoryByItemids(token, HistoryGetParams.builder()
 156:                     .fromEpoch(from).toEpoch(to).itemids(e.getValue()).history(e.getKey()).build());
 157:             for (HistoryPoint h : history) {
 158:                 byItem.computeIfAbsent(h.itemid(), k -> new ArrayList<>())
 159:                         .add(new SeriesPoint(toEpoch(h.timestamp()), toDouble(h.value())));
 160:             }
 161:         }
 162:         return byItem;
 163:     }
 164: 
 165:     private static List<String> distinctItemids(List<Cell> cells) {
 166:         LinkedHashSet<String> ids = new LinkedHashSet<>();
 167:         for (Cell c : cells) {
 168:             if (c.item != null) {
 169:                 ids.add(c.item.itemid());
 170:             }
 171:         }
 172:         return new ArrayList<>(ids);
 173:     }

 176:     public List<LatestValue> queryLatest(SourceRef src, CallerContext ctx, LatestQuery query) {
 177:         String token = sessionProvider.resolve(ctx);
 178:         List<LatestValue> out = new ArrayList<>();
 179:         try {
 180:             BatchResolver resolver = buildResolver(token, query.entities(), query.metrics());
 190:         for (String region : listRegion) {
 191:             tasks.add(() -> instancesForRegion(region, vpcIds));
 192:         }
 193:         List<Map<String, Object>> ec2s = new ArrayList<>();
 194:         for (List<Map<String, Object>> part : runParallel(tasks)) {
 195:             if (part != null) {
 196:                 ec2s.addAll(part);
 197:             }
 198:         }
 199:         return Map.of("items", ec2s);

 202:     /** ?⑥씪 由ъ쟾??EC2 ?곸꽭 紐⑸줉. */
 203:     private List<Map<String, Object>> instancesForRegion(String region, List<String> vpcIds) {
 204:         List<Map<String, Object>> ec2s = new ArrayList<>();
 205:         try {
 206:             List<Instance> instances = aws.describeInstances(region);
 207:             if (instances.isEmpty()) {
 208:                 return ec2s;
 209:             }
 210:             List<String> instanceIds = instances.stream().map(Instance::instanceId)
 211:                     .filter(java.util.Objects::nonNull).toList();
 212:             Set<String> vpcIdSet = new LinkedHashSet<>();
 213:             instances.forEach(i -> {
 214:                 if (i.vpcId() != null) {
 215:                     vpcIdSet.add(i.vpcId());
 216:                 }
 217:             });
 218: 
 219:             Map<String, String> statusMap = instanceStatuses(region, instanceIds);
 220:             Map<String, String> vpcNameMap = vpcNameMap(region, new ArrayList<>(vpcIdSet));
 221: 
 222:             for (Instance inst : instances) {
 223:                 if (vpcIds != null && (inst.vpcId() == null || !vpcIds.contains(inst.vpcId()))) {
 224:                     continue;
 225:                 }
 226:                 String vpcId = inst.vpcId() != null ? inst.vpcId() : "-";
 227:                 String name = inst.tags().stream().filter(t -> "Name".equals(t.key()))
 228:                         .map(t -> t.value()).findFirst().orElse("-");
 229: 
 230:                 Map<String, Object> item = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 231:                 item.put("instanceName", name);
 232:                 item.put("instanceId", inst.instanceId());
 233:                 item.put("instanceState", inst.state() != null ? inst.state().nameAsString() : "-");
 234:                 item.put("instanceType", inst.instanceTypeAsString() != null ? inst.instanceTypeAsString() : "-");
 235:                 item.put("statusCheck", statusMap.getOrDefault(inst.instanceId(), "-"));
 236:                 item.put("region", region);
 237:                 item.put("availabilityZone", inst.placement() != null ? inst.placement().availabilityZone() : "-");
 238:                 item.put("vpcName", vpcNameMap.getOrDefault(vpcId, vpcId));
 239:                 item.put("vpcId", vpcId);
 240:                 item.put("securityGroupName", inst.securityGroups().stream()
 241:                         .map(g -> g.groupName()).reduce((a, b) -> a + ", " + b).orElse("-"));
 242:                 // ?뚯뒪 留ㅽ듃由?뒪 而ㅻ쾭由ъ?(4媛? ??"?????몄뒪?댁뒪??硫붾え由?룸뵒?ㅽ겕媛 ?녿뒗媛"瑜?紐⑸줉???듯븳??
 243:                 item.put("monitoredBy", monitoredBy(inst, region));
 244:                 ec2s.add(item);
 245:             }
 246:         } catch (Exception e) {
 247:             log.error("Error get EC2 on region {}: {}", region, e.getMessage());
 248:         }
 249:         return ec2s;
 250:     }

 269:     private RegionInstances instancesWithStatus(String region) {
 270:         try {
 271:             List<Instance> instances = aws.describeInstances(region);
 272:             if (instances.isEmpty()) {
 273:                 return null;
 274:             }
 275:             List<String> instanceIds = instances.stream().map(Instance::instanceId)
 276:                     .filter(java.util.Objects::nonNull).toList();
 277:             return new RegionInstances(region, instances, instanceStatuses(region, instanceIds));
 278:         } catch (Exception e) {
 279:             log.error("Error get EC2 on region {}: {}", region, e.getMessage());
 280:             return null;
 281:         }

 284:     @Override
 285:     public Map<String, Object> summaryInstances(List<String> regions) {
 286:         List<String> listRegion = regions != null ? regions : getRegions();
 287:         List<Callable<RegionInstances>> tasks = new ArrayList<>();
 288:         for (String region : listRegion) {
 289:             tasks.add(() -> instancesWithStatus(region));
 290:         }
 291: 
 292:         Map<String, Integer> ec2ByRegion = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 293:         Map<String, Integer> ec2ByType = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 294:         Map<String, Integer> ec2ByState = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 295:         Map<String, Map<String, Integer>> ec2ByRegionAndStatus = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 296: 
 297:         // AWS ?몄텧? 蹂묐젹, 吏묎퀎???쒖꽌 蹂댁〈 ?⑥씪 ?ㅻ젅??硫깅벑)濡??섑뻾.
 298:         for (RegionInstances ri : runParallel(tasks)) {
 299:             if (ri == null) {
 300:                 continue;
 301:             }
 302:             ec2ByRegion.merge(ri.region(), ri.instances().size(), Integer::sum);
 303:             ec2ByRegionAndStatus.put(ri.region(), new LinkedHashMap<>());
 304:             for (Instance inst : ri.instances()) {
 305:                 ec2ByType.merge(inst.instanceTypeAsString(), 1, Integer::sum);
 306:                 ec2ByState.merge(inst.state() != null ? inst.state().nameAsString() : null, 1, Integer::sum);
 307:                 String status = ri.statusMap().get(inst.instanceId());
 308:                 ec2ByRegionAndStatus.get(ri.region()).merge(status, 1, Integer::sum);
 309:             }
 310:         }
 311: 
 312:         Map<String, Object> items = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 313:         items.put("ec2ByRegion", ec2ByRegion);
 314:         items.put("ec2ByType", ec2ByType);
 315:         items.put("ec2ByState", ec2ByState);
 316:         items.put("ec2ByRegionAndStatus", ec2ByRegionAndStatus);
 317:         return Map.of("items", items);
 318:     }
 319: 
 320:     /** 臾닿낵湲?媛쒖슂: EC2 ?몃깽?좊━? ?곹깭 ?먭?留??ъ슜?쒕떎. */
 321:     @Override
 322:     public Map<String, Object> overview(List<String> regions, TimeRange timeRange) {
 323:         List<String> targets = regions != null ? regions : getRegions();
 324:         List<Callable<OverviewRegion>> tasks = targets.stream()
 325:                 .map(region -> (Callable<OverviewRegion>) () -> overviewForRegion(region)).toList();
 326:         List<OverviewRegion> parts = runParallel(tasks);
 327:         List<Map<String, Object>> collection = new ArrayList<>();
 328:         List<Map<String, Object>> risks = new ArrayList<>();
 329:         int healthy=0, warning=0, critical=0, unobserved=0, stopped=0, total=0;
 330:         for (OverviewRegion p : parts) {
 331:             if (p == null) continue;
 332:             collection.add(p.collection()); risks.addAll(p.risks());
 333:             total += p.total; healthy += p.healthy; warning += p.warning; critical += p.critical; unobserved += p.unobserved; stopped += p.stopped;
 334:         }
 335:         Map<String,Integer> coverage = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 336:         coverage.put("zabbix-agent", 0); coverage.put("cloudwatch", 0);
 337:         coverage.put("unavailable", 0); coverage.put("unmonitored", 0);
 338:         for (OverviewRegion p : parts) {
 339:             if (p != null) { p.coverage().forEach((k, v) -> coverage.merge(k, v, Integer::sum)); }
 340:         }

codex
## 결함

1. **높음 — 만료된 매칭 캐시를 무기한 재사용해 오연결 가능**
   - `Ec2ZabbixMatcher.java:136-176`
   - 5분 TTL이 지난 뒤 `InventoryPort.listHosts()`가 실패하면 기존 `current`을 그대로 반환합니다. IP 재할당·호스트 삭제 후 Zabbix 장애가 지속되면, 과거 캐시의 host와 새 EC2를 계속 연결해 엉뚱한 agent CPU/메모리를 표시할 수 있습니다.
   - 만료 캐시는 조회 실패 시 버리고 `Optional.empty()`로 fail-closed 해야 합니다. 마지막 성공 캐시를 쓰려면 TTL 이내일 때만 허용해야 합니다.

2. **높음 — `useip=0`(DNS 접속) host가 IP 기반으로 오매칭될 수 있음**
   - `ZabbixInventoryAdapter.java:171-176`, `Ec2ZabbixMatcher.java:149-153`
   - 어댑터가 Zabbix interface의 `ip`만 `HostNetworkInterface.address`로 전달하고 `useip`/DNS 정보를 소실합니다. 따라서 DNS 접속 host의 단순 설정값 `ip`가 EC2 ENI 주소와 우연히 같으면 `IP_EXACT`로 연결됩니다.
   - 요청서의 6번은 “문서화된 제한”이 아니라 실제 미충족입니다. `useip`을 엔진 모델에 보존하거나, 최소한 DNS host는 매처 입력에서 제외해야 합니다.

3. **높음 — agent 품질 게이트가 CPU와 메모리 각각의 freshness를 보장하지 않음**
   - `CloudMetricsService.java:124-129`
   - `latestEpoch = max(cpu, memory)`라서 CPU가 3시간 전이고 메모리만 1분 전이어도 통과합니다. 이후 stale CPU와 최신 메모리를 `zabbix-agent` 소스로 반환하며 CW를 중단합니다.
   - CPU·메모리 모두 비어 있지 않고 각각 30분 이내인지 검사해야 합니다. `toPoints()`도 첫 `Series`만 사용하므로, 다중 Series 응답 시 freshness 판정 대상과 응답 대상이 달라질 수 있습니다 (`233-247`).

4. **중간 — stopped 인스턴스가 최대 30분간 `zabbix-agent`로 집계됨**
   - `AwsServiceImpl.java:51-63`
   - stopped 여부보다 agent freshness를 먼저 검사합니다. 방금 중지한 인스턴스는 마지막 Zabbix 수집이 30분 이내라 `zabbix-agent`로 분류됩니다.
   - 계약의 `stopped → unmonitored` 규칙에 맞게 stopped 판정을 먼저 해야 합니다.

5. **중간 — 커버리지의 CloudWatch 가용성 기준이 실제 metrics 경로와 불일치**
   - `AwsServiceImpl.java:60`, `CloudWatchAvailability.java:62-69`, `CloudMetricsService.java:157`
   - 목록/개요는 예산 잔여가 1회면 `cloudwatch`로 보지만, 실제 `/ec2/metrics`는 5개 metric 호출을 한 번에 예약해야 합니다. 예를 들어 잔여 3회면 커버리지는 `cloudwatch`, 상세는 즉시 `BUDGET_EXHAUSTED`가 됩니다.
   - 커버리지도 `CW_METRICS.size()` 기준을 공유해야 합니다.

6. **중간 — 예산 상한이 동시 요청에서 초과 가능**
   - `CloudWatchAvailability.java:43-56`, `CloudMetricsService.java:157-189`
   - “잔여 예산 확인”과 “호출 수 증가”가 분리돼 있습니다. 여러 상세 요청이 동시에 상한 직전의 동일한 used 값을 읽으면 모두 5개 호출을 수행하고 cap을 넘깁니다.
   - DB에서 조건부 증가(`calls + :n <= cap`)를 원자적으로 예약하고, 성공한 요청만 AWS 호출하도록 바꾸는 것이 안전합니다.

7. **중간 — 콜드 캐시에서 리전 병렬 요청 수만큼 Zabbix 전체 host 조회가 발생**
   - `Ec2ZabbixMatcher.java:136-172`, `AwsServiceImpl.java:322-326`
   - 개요는 리전별 병렬 처리인데 cache miss 시 각 스레드가 동시에 `listHosts(system)`을 호출할 수 있습니다. 기능 결과는 대체로 같지만 “전역 5분 캐시”의 비용 절감·부하 억제 목적을 충족하지 못합니다.
   - single-flight 동기화 또는 CAS 기반 로딩이 필요합니다.

8. **낮음 — FE 안내가 실제 제공 지표보다 과장됨**
   - `moveseyee_fe/src/locales/kr.json:471`, `en.json:471`
   - 안내문은 agent 설치 시 “메모리·디스크 사용률”을 수집한다고 하지만, 이번 계약과 구현의 agent 경로는 CPU·메모리만 채우고 디스크는 빈 배열입니다.
   - 안내를 CPU·메모리로 고치거나 디스크 구현 전까지 표현을 완화해야 합니다.

## 개선 제안

- 매칭 캐시는 TTL 만료 후 반드시 fail-closed하고, `useip=0` 구분 정보를 계약 모델까지 전달하세요.
- agent 선택은 CPU와 메모리 각각에 대해 “단일 확정 Series + 비어 있지 않음 + 개별 최신 point 30분 이내”를 보장하세요.
- `monitoredBy`의 stopped 우선순위와 CloudWatch 5-call 예산 기준을 `/ec2/metrics`와 공통 함수로 통일하세요.
- 예산은 조건부 원자 예약으로 전환하세요. 실패·AccessDenied 호출도 실제 발생한 수만큼 확정 기록하는 구조가 적합합니다.
- FE 안내문을 현재 1차 범위에 맞추세요.

## 요청서의 불안한 지점 판단

- **1. 단건 매칭만으로 유일성 확인 불가:** 여전히 구조적 한계입니다. 다만 `INSTANCE_ID_EXACT` 우선, 중복 ID/IP 거부로 위험은 상당히 낮췄습니다. `aws.instanceId` tag 운영을 필수 권장으로 승격하는 것이 좋습니다.
- **2. 시스템 컨텍스트의 권한 노출:** 구현은 설계대로입니다. cloud 화면의 `awsResources.view` 권한으로 별도 게이트하므로, 현재 계약상 허용된 제품 정책입니다.
- **3. 30분 freshness:** 하드코딩되어 있습니다. Zabbix 수집 주기가 1시간인 고객은 계속 CW fallback합니다. 설정화 또는 최소한 운영 문서의 명시가 필요합니다.
- **4. agent에서 네트워크/디스크 미제공:** 이번 개정 계약상 CPU·메모리만 제공하므로 구현은 맞습니다. 다만 UI 문구는 디스크까지 제공한다고 잘못 말합니다.
- **5. opted-in 리전 누락:** `AwsServiceImpl.java:107-112`는 여전히 `opt-in-not-required`만 반환합니다. `enabledRegionNames()`와 불일치하며, opted-in 리전이 목록·개요 기본 대상에서 빠집니다.
- **6. DNS host:** 실제로 미해결이며 위 2번 결함입니다.

## 테스트 공백

- 캐시 만료 후 inventory 실패 시 stale 매칭을 반환하지 않는 테스트
- `useip=0` host가 IP 매칭에서 제외되는 통합 테스트
- CPU만 stale / 메모리만 stale인 품질 게이트 테스트
- 다중 `Series` 응답의 처리 정책 테스트
- stopped + 최근 LAST_CHECK가 `unmonitored`가 되는 테스트
- 예산 잔여 1~4회에서 커버리지와 상세 응답의 일치 테스트
- 동시 요청에서 cap을 넘지 않는 repository 통합 테스트
- FE의 agent/CW/fallback 렌더링 및 “디스크 미제공” 안내 검증

## 총평

설계 §8의 핵심 구조(ENI 전체 주소, AGENT 제한, 시스템 컨텍스트, 4값 커버리지, 리전별 거부 캐시)는 대체로 반영됐습니다. 그러나 DNS host 오매칭, 만료 캐시 재사용, 개별 지표 freshness 누락은 잘못된 host 지표를 보여줄 수 있는 결함입니다. **수정 후 머지** 의견입니다.
hook: Stop
hook: Stop
hook: Stop Completed
hook: Stop Failed
tokens used
132,854
## 결함

1. **높음 — 만료된 매칭 캐시를 무기한 재사용해 오연결 가능**
   - `Ec2ZabbixMatcher.java:136-176`
   - 5분 TTL이 지난 뒤 `InventoryPort.listHosts()`가 실패하면 기존 `current`을 그대로 반환합니다. IP 재할당·호스트 삭제 후 Zabbix 장애가 지속되면, 과거 캐시의 host와 새 EC2를 계속 연결해 엉뚱한 agent CPU/메모리를 표시할 수 있습니다.
   - 만료 캐시는 조회 실패 시 버리고 `Optional.empty()`로 fail-closed 해야 합니다. 마지막 성공 캐시를 쓰려면 TTL 이내일 때만 허용해야 합니다.

2. **높음 — `useip=0`(DNS 접속) host가 IP 기반으로 오매칭될 수 있음**
   - `ZabbixInventoryAdapter.java:171-176`, `Ec2ZabbixMatcher.java:149-153`
   - 어댑터가 Zabbix interface의 `ip`만 `HostNetworkInterface.address`로 전달하고 `useip`/DNS 정보를 소실합니다. 따라서 DNS 접속 host의 단순 설정값 `ip`가 EC2 ENI 주소와 우연히 같으면 `IP_EXACT`로 연결됩니다.
   - 요청서의 6번은 “문서화된 제한”이 아니라 실제 미충족입니다. `useip`을 엔진 모델에 보존하거나, 최소한 DNS host는 매처 입력에서 제외해야 합니다.

3. **높음 — agent 품질 게이트가 CPU와 메모리 각각의 freshness를 보장하지 않음**
   - `CloudMetricsService.java:124-129`
   - `latestEpoch = max(cpu, memory)`라서 CPU가 3시간 전이고 메모리만 1분 전이어도 통과합니다. 이후 stale CPU와 최신 메모리를 `zabbix-agent` 소스로 반환하며 CW를 중단합니다.
   - CPU·메모리 모두 비어 있지 않고 각각 30분 이내인지 검사해야 합니다. `toPoints()`도 첫 `Series`만 사용하므로, 다중 Series 응답 시 freshness 판정 대상과 응답 대상이 달라질 수 있습니다 (`233-247`).

4. **중간 — stopped 인스턴스가 최대 30분간 `zabbix-agent`로 집계됨**
   - `AwsServiceImpl.java:51-63`
   - stopped 여부보다 agent freshness를 먼저 검사합니다. 방금 중지한 인스턴스는 마지막 Zabbix 수집이 30분 이내라 `zabbix-agent`로 분류됩니다.
   - 계약의 `stopped → unmonitored` 규칙에 맞게 stopped 판정을 먼저 해야 합니다.

5. **중간 — 커버리지의 CloudWatch 가용성 기준이 실제 metrics 경로와 불일치**
   - `AwsServiceImpl.java:60`, `CloudWatchAvailability.java:62-69`, `CloudMetricsService.java:157`
   - 목록/개요는 예산 잔여가 1회면 `cloudwatch`로 보지만, 실제 `/ec2/metrics`는 5개 metric 호출을 한 번에 예약해야 합니다. 예를 들어 잔여 3회면 커버리지는 `cloudwatch`, 상세는 즉시 `BUDGET_EXHAUSTED`가 됩니다.
   - 커버리지도 `CW_METRICS.size()` 기준을 공유해야 합니다.

6. **중간 — 예산 상한이 동시 요청에서 초과 가능**
   - `CloudWatchAvailability.java:43-56`, `CloudMetricsService.java:157-189`
   - “잔여 예산 확인”과 “호출 수 증가”가 분리돼 있습니다. 여러 상세 요청이 동시에 상한 직전의 동일한 used 값을 읽으면 모두 5개 호출을 수행하고 cap을 넘깁니다.
   - DB에서 조건부 증가(`calls + :n <= cap`)를 원자적으로 예약하고, 성공한 요청만 AWS 호출하도록 바꾸는 것이 안전합니다.

7. **중간 — 콜드 캐시에서 리전 병렬 요청 수만큼 Zabbix 전체 host 조회가 발생**

   - 안내를 CPU·메모리로 고치거나 디스크 구현 전까지 표현을 완화해야 합니다.
